散射与动力学演化1 篇

Eccentricities and inclinations are decoupled in super-Earth systems

Sacha Kuhn, Antoine C. Petit, Federico Mogavero, Aurélien Crida · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过数值模拟和动力学分析,发现在稳定的低质量多行星系统中,偏心率角动量亏损(AMD)和倾角AMD作为准守恒量,其相对变化通常低于1%,这种守恒性是系统稳定性的普遍特征。研究表明,尽管在摄动函数展开中偏心率和倾角存在强耦合,但它们的AMD演化在低AMD区域表现出很强的解耦性,即使系统进入大尺度随机混沌区域,这些准守恒量的破缺也较为缓慢。这些准守恒量的发现为将观测到的行星系统结构与其形成过程相联系提供了新的途径。
英文原摘要
Recent work on the longterm evolution of the inner Solar System has shown that the inclination contribution to the Angular Momentum Deficit (AMD) is almost constant even though inclinations and eccentricities are highly coupled in expansions of the perturbing function. We wish to characterize the conservation of the eccentricity AMD and inclination AMD, respectively describing the average eccentricity and mutual inclination of planetary orbits, beyond the case of the Solar System. We aim at showing that this conservation is a general feature of the stable planetary systems dynamics, in particular, in the context of low mass multiplanetary systems. We perform a series of numerical simulations using both N-body and secular codes to study the dynamics over billions of orbits. Taking as a template four equal mass Super-Earth with non uniform orbital spacing, we consider sets of systems with different AMD and use the orbital elements evolution to assess the conservation of inclination and eccentricity AMD components. For stable systems, the inclination AMD and eccentricity AMD relative variation is typically below 1%, making them quasi-integrals of motion. At low AMD, despite short Lyapunov timescales, the secular diffusion is confined and the dynamics almost quasi-periodic. As the AMD increases, the emergence of large scale secular chaos only weakly affects the conservation of the quasi-integrals, which breaks when systems become fully unstable. The eccentricity and inclination evolution (as measured by the variation of their AMD contributions) are largely decoupled for low mass multiplanetary systems. Combined with the weak diffusion of the Laplace-Lagrange modes at low AMD, these findings paint a picture where the secular dynamics are not necessarily ergodic. The uncovering of these quasi-integrals opens a new path to relate the observed planetary architecture to its formation.
secular chaos
系外行星观测9 篇

CORALIE Radial Velocity Search for Companions around Evolved Stars (CASCADES) VI. Revised masses and homogeneous estimation of the stellar parameters of 583 red giants

Emile Fontanet, Stéphane Udry, Damien Ségransan, Patrick Eggenberger et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过TESS光曲线提取583颗红巨星的震荡频率参数，结合SED拟合和小行星震荡标度关系重新测定了CASCADES巡天样本的恒星参数，特别是恒星质量。研究发现使用震荡方法得到的质量比传统等龄线拟合方法低15-20%（中位差为-0.35太阳质量），这对低金属丰度恒星尤为显著，使样本质量中位数从2.11太阳质量下降至1.65太阳质量。这些修正后的恒星质量为后续行星发生率与宿主星质量关系的研究奠定了基础，同时揭示了演化恒星质量测定中存在的系统性不确定性。
英文原摘要
Giant-star radial-velocity surveys allow us to study how the exoplanet population varies with stellar mass and evolution. However, obtaining accurate estimates of stellar masses for such stars is challenging, as methods like isochrone fitting are prone to large errors due to the degeneracy associated with the closeness of isochrones. We provide a homogeneous re-derivation of the stellar parameters of the CASCADES giant-star survey. This includes parameters obtained from spectral energy distribution fitting, as well as an update of the stellar masses using asteroseismology. We use TESS light curves to measure the frequency of maximum oscillation power, $ν_{\rm max}$, for all the stars when applicable. We re-extract the stellar radii of the survey by fitting broadband photometry with ARIADNE, a spectral energy distribution (SED) fitting package, using priors from spectroscopy for the effective temperature, metallicity and $\log g$. By using scaling relations, we compute revised estimates of the stellar masses of targets with a $ν_{\rm max}$ measurement. We obtain new estimates of mass for 583 out of the 640 stars of the survey. These revised masses are typically 15 to 20% lower (median difference of $-0.35\,M_\odot$) than the ones obtained by isochrone fitting, in particular for low-metallicity stars for which this effect is stronger. These new masses impact the overall mass distribution of the sample, whose median decreases from $2.11\,M_\odot$ to $1.65\,M_\odot$. These revised masses provide the basis for future occurrence-rate analyses aiming to study the link between host-star mass and planet occurrence. They also highlight the uncertainties associated with the determination of masses for evolved stars.
radial velocity · occurrence rate

A homogeneous mass campaign of transiting sub-Neptunes around FGK stars

Pierrot Lamontagne, Rafael Luque, Pedro Figueira, Carlos Cifuentes et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究对开普勒和K2任务观测的12个亚海王星系统进行了同质化质量测量，使用多条谱线(HIRES、HARPS-N和ESPRESSO)和统一的贝叶斯框架，通过四条独立提取管道和交叉验证堆叠消除单一管道依赖的系统偏差。研究获得了4颗行星的高精度质量(>3σ)和密度测量，以及其他15个信号的上限约束，揭示了传统单管道分析可能忽视的显著径向速度系统误差。
英文原摘要
Sub-Neptunes are among the most common planets in our Galaxy, but are still poorly understood. Addressing their origins and compositions requires precise bulk densities, but literature values come from studies using different radial velocity (RV) extraction methods, stellar activity treatments, and statistical frameworks, which introduces systematic biases that can lead to spurious conclusions in population-level analyses. We conducted a systematic and homogeneous mass-measurement campaign for 12 sub-Neptune systems observed by Kepler and K2, selected for their absent or poorly constrained masses ($σ_{M_p}/M_p > 25\%$). Using new HIRES, HARPS-N, and ESPRESSO spectroscopy as well as archival data, we applied a unified hierarchical Bayesian framework with homogeneous data curation. For HARPS-N and ESPRESSO, we applied four independent extraction pipelines (CCF, SERVAL, LBL, sBART) and combined the posteriors via leave-one-out cross-validation stacking, so that no mass would hinge on a single pipeline. A preferred noise model for each target was selected through a uniform Bayesian procedure. We obtained $>3σ$ masses for four transiting planets: Kepler-21 b ($5.66^{+0.65}_{-0.96} M_\oplus$, $7.61^{+0.96}_{-1.28}$ g cm$^{-3}$), K2-66 b ($17.9 \pm 3.2 M_\oplus$, $4.69 \pm 1.31$ g cm$^{-3}$), Kepler-538 b ($10.8^{+2.4}_{-2.8} M_\oplus$), and K2-182 b ($20.1^{+4.5}_{-7.8} M_\oplus$). We also provide updated masses for the non-transiting giants Kepler-65 e and Kepler-129 d, and upper limits for the remaining 15 signals, including the first constraints for K2-184, K2-244, and K2-269. No new non-transiting companion was detected in any system. The semi-amplitudes of the four reductions span a median of $0.75σ$ of the stacked posterior, and up to $2.7σ$, a systematic that a single-pipeline analysis would ignore.
radial velocity

Kepler-404: A near-resonant pair of sub-Neptunes. Reinterpreting a false-positive signal with the novel detection method RIVERS.radon

Yannick Eyholzer, Marylyn Rosenqvist, Adrien Leleu, Vitaliy Kinakh et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对凯普勒-404系统中的假阳性信号,提出了克服过渡时间变化(TTV)影响的新型行星检测方法RIVERS.radon,该方法基于二维光变曲线表示和局部周期解的相关性分析。通过光动力学拟合,确认KOI-2768.03信号属于Kepler-404b,揭示该系统为接近5:4共振的类地行星对,具有低密度、近共面轨道等典型近共振特征,但轨道位置与哈密顿平面的共振平衡点存在显著偏离,暗示其形成演化历史不同于传统的原行星盘内共振捕获机制。
英文原摘要
According to the current understanding of planet formation and evolution, (near)-resonant systems should be a common occurrence in nature. However, pairs of planets with period ratios close to commensurability are rarely observed. In this paper, we discuss how observational bias caused by transit timing variations (TTVs) can affect the detection of low signal-to-noise ratio (S/N) planets in transit surveys, playing a role in the lack of representation of these systems. We present an alternative detection method, RIVERS.radon, which overcomes the effect of TTVs on planet detectability by not relying on an exact periodic solution for the transit signal. It leverages 2D representations of the light curve and approximates the transits with a set of local periodic solutions with the use of 2D cross-correlation. We present an application of RIVERS.radon to the near-resonant system Kepler-404, showing that the signal of the false-positive candidate KOI-2768.03 belongs to the planet Kepler-404b and that it is in a near 5:4 resonance with its companion Kepler-404c, causing TTVs of amplitudes 117.64 and 110.09 minutes, respectively. With a photodynamical fit, we retrieve a pair of similar planets, respectively of radii $1.68^{+0.09}_{-0.09}$ and $1.69^{+0.10}_{-0.10}\,R_\oplus$ and of masses $1.91^{+0.41}_{-0.33}$ and $2.34^{+0.47}_{-0.40}\,M_\oplus$. We find that these planets exhibit typical (near)-resonant features: their densities are lower than the bulk population of exoplanets, and their orbits are nearly coplanar. Interestingly, the planets are found outside the resonance but at a significant distance from the equilibrium point of the $(X_{\rm res}, δ)$ plane of the Hamiltonian, which is consistent with post-disk instability but somewhat at odds with the observed coplanarity.
transit timing · transit survey

The evolution of discs and the epoch of formation of giant planets around low-mass stars

Raffaele Gratton, Elisabetta Rigliaco · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过比较不同恒星质量周围原行星盘内大颗粒的耗尽特征，探讨巨行星形成的观测迹象。我们发现，太阳型恒星周围约50%的盘显示尘埃过滤的证据，而低质量恒星（早期M型星）周围仅约9%的盘显示此特征，这与低质量恒星周围巨行星形成频率较低的观测事实相符。我们的结果表明，只有质量足够大的行星核心才能有效地过滤大颗粒，而海王星质量及以下的行星通常无法产生明显的尘埃过滤特征。
英文原摘要
Large-grain depletion in the inner discs of young stellar systems provides a potential observational signature of dust filtration by forming giant-planet cores. In a previous study, we found that $0.50\pm0.10$ of prominent protoplanetary discs around solar-type stars ($0.7$--$1.5$~\Msun) with ages of 2--8~Myr show evidence for a depletion of large grains inside the snow line. Such a depletion may arise when a sufficiently massive planetary core acts as a barrier to the inward drift of large grains, filtering them from the inner disc. If this interpretation is correct, the frequency of dust-filtration signatures should decrease towards lower stellar masses, in accordance with the lower occurrence rate of giant planets around low-mass stars. Extending our analysis to lower-mass stars, we found that only $0.09\pm0.09$ of discs around stars with masses of $0.30$--$0.58$~\Msun, corresponding to the progenitors of main-sequence early M-type stars, show evidence of grain filtration. This fraction is lower than that measured for solar-type stars and is consistent with the lower frequency of giant planets around M-type stars. We also found that discs exhibiting evidence of filtration tend to have relatively massive outer dust reservoirs, with dust masses exceeding $20$~\MEarth. Conversely, the absence of filtration signatures in lower-mass discs suggests that Neptune-mass and lower-mass planets, despite being common around low-mass stars, may generally be unable to filter large grains efficiently.
occurrence rate

The rotation period of the near-Earth comet (875163) 1998 SH2 and the limits of a single-apparition phase curve

Javier Licandro, Miguel R. Alarcón, Miquel Serra-Ricart · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过TTT2多夜密集测光导出近地彗星(875163) 1998 SH2的自转周期P = 6.970 ± 0.020小时，光变曲线呈显著双峰特征表明该天体为伸长体。受观测相角范围狭窄(3.7-9.3°)的限制，IAU标准斜率参数H,G系统无法得到物理约束，但表观相位梯度β_app = 0.074 ± 0.005 mag/deg处于木星族彗星分布上限，结合弱活跃性活动特征，该值可视为内禀表面相位斜率的上限。在木星族彗星核无或极弱对日面增亮的群体先验假设下，相位行为最优描述为线性关系，给出暂定光度H值约20.9-21.0。
英文原摘要
The near-Earth object (875163) 1998 SH2 was recently shown to be a weakly active comet: its 1998-2025 astrometry reveals a non-gravitational acceleration too large to be radiative in origin, and large-aperture imaging during its 2025 apparition revealed a faint tail (Farnocchia et al. 2026). No rotation period had been reported. We derive one from dense TTT2 photometry obtained on five nights of the same apparition: from 2392 calibrated measurements we find a synodic rotation period P = 6.970 +/- 0.020 h. The folded light curve is markedly bimodal (A2/A1 ~ 6.4) with a peak-to-peak amplitude of ~0.18 mag, implying a/b >~ 1.18. Combined with the published radar Doppler bandwidth, the period is consistent with an elongated body whose rotation the radar alone could not constrain. Over the narrow observed phase-angle range (3.7-9.3 deg) the IAU H,G and H,G1,G2 slope parameters are not physically constrained. The inter-night magnitude gradient, beta_app = 0.074 +/- 0.005 mag/deg, is steeper than the default G=0.15 and lies in the upper part of the Jupiter-family comet (JFC) distribution (median 0.046; Kokotanekova et al. 2017); because the object was weakly and increasingly active during the run, we treat this value as an upper limit to the intrinsic surface phase slope. Under the population prior of little or no opposition surge, as found in available JFC nucleus phase curves, the phase behaviour is best described by a linear law, giving a provisional H_Lum(1,1,0) ~ 20.9-21.0 over 3.7-9.3 deg in the Luminance band.
astrometry

A New Population of Jovian Exoplanets Suitable For Direct Imaging with the Habitable Worlds Observatory, the Roman Coronagraph, and ELTs Identified from HARPS Radial-Velocity Data and Hipparcos-Gaia Astrometry

Jie Li, Thayne Currie, Yiting Li, Mona El Morsy et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究联合分析HARPS径向速度数据和Hipparcos-Gaia天体测量数据，识别并约束长周期木星型系外行星的动力学性质，新确认14颗行星（质量0.04-6.1木星质量，半长轴0.1-12.6天文单位），并约束了32颗已知径向速度行星的轨道和质量。研究表明该样本中有5个目标可被Roman日冕仪在反射光中探测，28个可被宜居世界观测站观测，6个可被E-ELT/METIS在热辐射中探测，证明了径向速度和天体测量数据联合分析在发现和表征长周期巨行星中的优势，并为宜居世界观测站的类地行星成像提供了重要的目标样本。
英文原摘要
We jointly analyze archival radial-velocity data from the HARPS spectrograph and astrometry from Hipparcos and Gaia to identify and constrain the dynamical properties of new and known long-period jovian exoplanets. Our search yields 14 new confirmed planets with masses of 0.04--6.1 $M_{\rm Jup}$ and semimajor axes of 0.1--12.6 au. Planets include a super-Jupiter in a near-Saturn-like orbit producing a strong astrometric acceleration (HIP 669 b), an eccentric Jupiter around a K star with a modest acceleration (HIP 39039 b), a 74-day-orbit warm Neptune (HIP 64550 b), and a giant planet around a Tier A Habitable Worlds Observatory target that may endanger any habitable zone Earth (HIP 17378 b). Three systems show evidence for candidate planets requiring additional data for confirmation. Our modeling also constrains the dynamical masses and orbits for 32 previously known radial-velocity-discovered planets. Orbits, masses, and ages for our sample show that five targets are potentially detectable in reflected light with the Roman Coronagraph, 28 should be imageable in reflected light with the Habitable Worlds Observatory, and six may be detectable in thermal emission with E-ELT/METIS. These results demonstrate the power of combining archival radial-velocity data with astrometry to discover and characterize long-period giant planets, a capability that will only grow with Gaia DR4. It provides a new sample of targets for future direct-imaging observations, and shows a path forward for timely vetting of targets for exo-Earth imaging with the Habitable Worlds Observatory.
direct imaging · radial velocity · astrometry · astrometric

Revisiting TOI-2120 b: Updated mass and radius constraints on a sub-Neptune orbiting an M4 dwarf

Rachel B. Fernandes, Andrea S. J. Lin, Paul Robertson, Shubham Kanodia et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过结合TESS存档数据、地面测光和新的径向速度观测，更新了环绕M4矮星的短周期亚海王星TOI-2120 b的质量和半径测量。结果表明该行星半径为2.31±0.7地球半径（大于前期报告值），质量为5.81(+2.94/-2.69)地球质量，密度为2.62(+1.37/-1.22) g cm⁻³，将其位置定位于富水区域。这一结果对理解低质量恒星周围行星的组成和半径谷演化具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
We present updated mass and radius measurements of TOI-2120 b, a short-period (5.80 days) planet orbiting a mid-M dwarf identified by the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). TOI-2120 b was previously validated through transit photometry and high-resolution imaging, with a radius near the upper boundary of the radius valley around low-mass stars. We refined the planetary properties by combining archival TESS and ground-based photometry with new transit observations from the ARC 3.5m and Red Buttes Observatory (RBO) telescopes and new precision RV measurements obtained with the Habitable-zone Planet Finder (HPF) spectrograph. Our joint analysis yields a radius of $2.31\pm0.7$ Rearth, larger than previously reported. We also measure a planetary mass of $5.81^{+2.94}_{-2.69}$ Mearth at 2$σ$, corresponding to a bulk density of $2.62^{+1.37}_{-1.22}$ g cm$^{-3}$, positioning TOI-2120 b toward the water-rich region predicted by isocomposition models. Additional RV observations will be needed to further constrain its mass and composition.
transiting exoplanet

Refining the Architecture of the Quadruple Pre-main-sequence System HD 98800: Improved Orbits, Dynamical Masses, and a Transiting Circumbinary Disk

Guillermo Torres, John D. Monnier, Gail H. Schaefer, Andrew F. Boden et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究对近邻TW Hya星协中的四星系统HD 98800进行了长期径向速度监测（覆盖36年以上）和干涉测量观测，精确约束了四颗恒星的轨道和动力学质量（精度≤2.6%），其结果与现有恒星演化模型相符。研究发现该系统的B双星携带一个极向定向的环双星盘，同时捕捉到A双星正好通过盘后方的罕见对齐现象，这将持续约十年，为探测潜在的行星形成盘的结构和成分提供了独特机会。本工作的精确轨道和质量约束为该系统的后续动力学研究奠定了坚实基础。
英文原摘要
We present new spectroscopic and interferometric observations of the pre-main-sequence hierarchical quadruple system HD 98800, a member of the nearby TW Hya association. Our radial velocity monitoring extends the total coverage to over 36 years, sampling the periastron passage in the $\sim$240-yr outer orbit. By combining these velocities with new and archival interferometric observations of both inner binaries (A and B), we significantly refine the system orbits and constrain the dynamical masses of all four stars to $\leq$ 2.6 percent. The resulting stellar properties (masses, effective temperatures, and absolute magnitudes) are found to agree well with current stellar evolution models. Notably, binary B hosts a known circumbinary disk in a polar orientation. Our interferometric imaging, as well as ground-based photometry by others, capture binary A currently passing directly behind the disk, a striking alignment given the outer orbit's long period. We use the photometry to infer the optical extinction produced by the disk material. The eclipse is expected to last about a decade, and offers an unprecedented opportunity to probe the structure and composition of the potentially planet-forming disk. Our refined orbits and component masses provide a robust dynamical framework to support ongoing and future studies of this unique event.
radial velocity

orbitize! v4: Joint Orbit Fitting with Gaia DR4 Astrometry and Flexible Analytic Linear Marginalization

Clarissa R. Do Ó, Eshel Dror, Sarah Blunt, Vanessa P. Bailey et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
orbitize! v4是一个用于行星和恒星伴星贝叶斯轨道拟合的Python程序包,支持相对位置、径向速度和绝对位置测量的联合分析,并提供多种后验采样方法。本版本的主要改进包括与Gaia DR4星历位置数据的联合拟合能力,以及实现了高效的OFTeI算法以加速轨道参数反演。这些功能扩展使该工具包能够更准确地约束已解析和未解析系统中伴星的轨道动力学参数。
英文原摘要
orbitize! is a Python package for Bayesian orbit fitting of planetary and stellar companions (Blunt et al., 2020). It provides a unified framework for fitting relative astrometry, radial velocities, and absolute astrometry, supporting analyses of both resolved and unresolved systems. Available posterior sampling methods include parallel-tempered Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) through ptemcee (Foreman-Mackey et al., 2013; Vousden et al., 2016) and nested sampling through dynesty (Speagle, 2020), nautilus (Lange, 2023), and pymultinest (Buchner et al., 2014; Feroz et al., 2019). This paper accompanies the release of orbitize! version 4.0 and describes the functionality added since version 3.0 (Blunt et al., 2024), with a particular focus on joint orbit fitting with Gaia DR4 epoch astrometry and the implementation of the Orbits for the Extremely Impatient (OFTeI) algorithm (Blunt et al. 2026b, in preparation).
astrometry
行星形成与盘演化5 篇

Dynamical conditions for tilting Neptune during planetary migration

Andrea Moretti, Giacomo Lari · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过数值模拟海王星在行星迁移期间的自转轴动力学,探究原始卫星系统是否能触发自旋-轨道共振并导致其自转轴倾斜。研究发现,当原始卫星系统总质量≥4×10⁻⁴M_N且具有特定的质量分布和轨道配置(如内侧卫星质量5×10⁻⁵M_N、半长轴35R_N,外侧卫星质量3.5×10⁻⁴M_N、半长轴100R_N)时,可成功激发共振并倾斜海王星;该卫星系统在行星迁移不稳定阶段若未遭遇过于接近的引力相互作用可得以保存,外侧卫星的后续喷出则固定了海王星的现有倾角,为特里同俘获提供了合适的初始配置。
英文原摘要
Giant planets are expected to form with near-zero obliquities, so that their current tilts must be the result of some post-formation processes. Capture into spin-orbit resonances has been shown to provide a viable mechanism for explaining the current obliquities of Jupiter, Saturn, and Uranus. We aim to determine whether specific configurations of a primordial satellite system of Neptune could have enabled the tilting of its spin axis during planetary migration. We modeled Neptune's secular spin-axis dynamics, including the presence of a primordial satellite system that enhanced the planet's precession constant. We numerically explored the conditions on the primordial satellite system that could trigger a spin-orbit resonance. We identified an open set in the parameter space that led to a successful tilting of Neptune. In particular, the total mass of the primordial system must have been $\geq 4\times 10^{-4}\,M_N$. A representative configuration consists of an inner satellite of mass $5\times 10 ^{-5}\,M_N$ and semi-major axis $35\,R_N$, and an outer satellite of mass $3.5\times 10 ^{-4}\,M_N$ and semi-major axis $100\,R_N$. Such a system could have survived during the unstable phase of the planetary migration if the gravitational encounters between Neptune and Uranus were not too close. The eventual ejection of the outer moon, possibly due to the encounter with a planetesimal, froze Neptune's obliquity to its current value. Neptune's current obliquity can be attained during planetary migration if the planet initially possessed a satellite system with a suitable architecture. The required satellite properties are not far from theoretical predictions and are consistent with recent scenarios proposed for the tilting of Uranus. Once the outer moon was ejected, the system matched a configuration suitable for the following capture of Triton and its fast evolution toward its current orbit.
planetesimal

From residuals to warps: Probing disk geometry with gas kinematics

C. N. Kimmig, G. P. Rosotti, A. J. Winter, G. Lodato et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过ALMA CO发射线观测的气体运动学残差来推断原行星盘的翘曲几何结构，采用辐射转移模拟方法对该方法的适用性和局限进行了系统测试。研究发现该方法对近面向盘和小翘曲角度（几度）的系统有较好的恢复效果，但在盘倾角>40°、发射体高度大或翘曲变化急剧的情况下失效，同时径向和竖直方向的气体运动会引入虚假的翘曲信号。这些结果为利用气体运动学诊断原行星盘三维结构和动力学演化提供了重要的观测约束。
英文原摘要
Evidence of shadowing among protoplanetary disks suggests that warped protoplanetary disks are widespread, making it crucial to constrain their three-dimensional shape in order to understand their dynamical evolution and origin. Yet, inferring this warp shape from observations is challenging, as the typical signatures of a warp, especially shadows, are often degenerate with the warp shape. Kinematics of disks may provide a method to recover the shape by interpreting the residuals from a Keplerian disk model as local changes in inclination and position angle. In this work, we test its applicability and limitations. We perform radiative transfer simulations of disks with a parametrized warp shape to create synthetic maps of $^{12}\mathrm{CO}$ rotational line emission at ALMA wavelengths and compare the fitted warp with the true warp shape. We find that the warp shape can be recovered for nearly face-on disks with small warp misalignments of a few degrees, even without knowledge about disk properties such as emission height. The overall warp shape remains traceable even at moderate spectral resolutions up to $0.8\,\mathrm{km/s}$. However, we identify regimes where the method breaks down, including disks with an inclination higher than $40^\circ$, a high emission height of the tracer, and sharp warp transitions. In addition, radial and vertical motions can introduce apparent warps and twists, motivating extensions of the method to account for these effects.
protoplanetary disk

A Warped Dust Ring in the CQ Tau Disk

Shu Ishibashi, Masayuki Yamaguchi, Takayuki Muto, Naomi Hirano et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究利用ALMA 1.3 mm观测数据分析了CQ Tau原行星盘的尘埃结构，通过椭圆拟合发现盘的位置角和倾角沿径向变化，表明存在从内边界(~21-26 au)的~4°倾角逐渐减小到外边界(~60 au)的~1°倾角的尘埃翘曲结构。研究采用翘曲环模型成功解释了观测到的结构特征，而共面环模型则无法再现这些变化，从而确立CQ Tau作为翘曲尘埃盘的典范地位。这项工作为理解三维盘结构与行星形成的关联提供了重要观测约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract The diversity of planetary orbital architectures calls for observational constraints on radial variations in disk-plane orientation, or warps, yet such variations in dust disks remain poorly characterized. CQ Tau is a promising target because its misaligned inner disk and candidate gas warp suggest a radially extended warp. We present the ALMA 1.3 mm dust continuum image reconstructed from the real part of the visibilities, extracting the centro-symmetric disk structure. At an effective spatial resolution of 0 . ″ 052 × 0 . ″ 042 (7.8 × 6.3 au), the image reveals a bright main ring at radii of ∼20–70 au and an unresolved compact emission component tracing the inner disk around the central star. Ellipse fits centered on the stellar position to contours tracing the inner and outer edges of the ring reveal radial variations in position angles and inclinations. At the inner edge (∼21–26 au), the position angle and inclination span 214 ∘ –233 ∘ and 23 ∘ –39 ∘ , respectively, while they span 231 ∘ –235 ∘ and 33 ∘ –36 ∘ at the outer edge (∼58–67 au). Whereas a coplanar ring toy model fails to reproduce such variations, a warped ring model with the local tilt decreasing from ∼4 ∘ at r = 20 au to ∼1 ∘ at 60 au reproduces them. The high-pass-filtered real-part image reveals a pair of opposed curved negative residuals. Similar features appear only in the warped ring model, further supporting the dust warp. These results establish CQ Tau as an example of a warped dust disk and an important laboratory for investigating the connection between three-dimensional disk geometry and planet formation.

The X-ray catalogue of FGK stars within 10 pc: The coronal temperature-brightness relation explained with the RTV scaling law

M. M. Bennedik, B. Stelzer, M. Caramazza, S. Orlando et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究基于Gaia数据构建了10pc内FGK型主序星的X射线目录(60颗星,X射线完整性95%),通过SRG/eROSITA、XMM-Newton和ROSAT数据分析,导出了日冕温度与X射线表面流量的关系。研究发现该温度-亮度关系的小散度可由日冕磁环的Rosner-Tucker-Vaiana(RTV标度律)自然解释,揭示了不同恒星日冕结构中磁环长度与填充因子比值的普适标度关系,同时识别了包括Maunder极小值候选星在内的异常恒星。这些结果对理解恒星磁动力、系外行星大气丧失和原行星盘蒸发等过程具有重要启示。
英文原摘要
A comprehensive measurement of stellar X-ray emission has important implications for our understanding of stellar dynamos, exoplanet atmosphere loss, and evaporation of protoplanetary disks. However, the samples discussed in the literature are often biased and the X-ray properties reported in published catalogues are often not homogeneously derived. We present a volume-limited catalogue of X-ray detections of the FGK-type main-sequence stars within 10,pc, which properly accounts for stellar multiplicity and considers the instrumental effect of optical loading of the X-ray detectors. The FGK,10pc sample derives from a recent Gaia -based catalogue of all stars in a sky volume of 10,pc around the Sun from which we extract all stars of spectral type F, G, and K. To build the X-ray catalogue, we retrieved and analysed all observations from SRG (Spectrum Roentgen Gamma)/eROSITA and XMM-Newton for stars in the FGK,10pc sample, and we cross-matched the sample with ROSAT catalogues. After accounting for optical loading, flares, and multiplicity, we fitted thermal plasma models to the X-ray spectra to derive X-ray fluxes and coronal temperatures. The FGK,10pc sample comprises 60 stars and has an X-ray completeness of 95%. We present a detailed investigation of the relation between coronal temperature and X-ray surface flux and we find the result to be consistent with the ranges covered by different types of coronal magnetic structures observed on the Sun. The lower end of the temperature-brightness relation is defined by the Maunder minimum star HD,166620 and three other stars whose positions identify them as possible Maunder minimum candidates. We identify systematic differences between the temperature-brightness law derived from XMM-Newton and eROSITA data that we attribute to their different instrumental response functions. For the first time, we study the impact of variability, both short-term flaring and long-term activity cycles, on the evolution of coronal temperature along with X-ray brightness. With the exception of three stars, a remarkably small scatter is observed in the temperature-brightness relation across the whole FGK,10pc sample. A natural explanation of the empirical kT-F_!̊m X relation is provided by the Rosner-Tucker-Vaiana (RTV) scaling laws defined for solar hydrostatic magnetic loops. The small spread of the observed relation indicates a universal scaling between the length and filling factor of coronal loops. Three observed outliers to the relation are stars that deviate from this invariance of the ratio of loop length to filling factor for an unknown reason.

Revisiting the protoplanet candidates in MWC 758. A panchromatic view of the system with high-contrast imaging from LBT

M. C. Vega-Pallauta, D. Mesa, S. Antoniucci, C. Desgrange et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究采用LBT望远镜的三台高对比成像仪器(SHARK-VIS、SHARK-NIR和LMIRCam)对MWC 758系统进行了多波段观测,旨在通过光学到中红外波段的全面数据来探测该双螺旋结构原行星盘中的原行星候选体及其吸积特征。观测结果未在先前报告的行星伴星位置检测到点源,并通过Hα观测提供了迄今最深的吸积光度上限,表明木星质量伴星仅在吸积率≤10⁻⁸M_Jup/yr时才可探测。结果否定了螺旋臂内的MWC 758 b候选体,而与螺旋臂外的MWC 758 c为行星质量天体的假设相符。
英文原摘要
Structures in protoplanetary discs can be the result of interactions between one or more forming planets and the disc, making them natural laboratories to test planet formation theories. The Herbig Ae star MWC 758 in the Taurus star-forming region hosts a protoplanetary disc with a double-spiral structure that has been hypothesised to have been induced by one or more companions. With this work, we aim to provide a comprehensive view of the disc across optical, near-infrared, and mid-infrared wavelengths and to confirm two companion candidates in the MWC 758 system reported in the literature. By comparing our detection limits with those from both previous detections and non-detections, we sought to reconcile the conflicting evidence and provide constraints on the masses and accretion rates of these protoplanet candidates. We present high-contrast imaging observations obtained with three instruments at the Large Binocular Telescope (LBT), SHARK-VIS (Hα, 0.6563,μm), SHARK-NIR (H band, 1.65,μm), and LMIRCam (L' band, 3.8,μm), in order to search for accretion signatures and thermal emission from potential companions. Our observations resolved the disc's spiral arms and substructures, but we did not detect any point-like sources at the locations of previously detected planetary companion candidates. Our Hα observations provide the deepest accretion luminosity limits to date for this system, and we show that a Jup protoplanet outside the spiral arms would only be detectable if accreting at $ M -8 , $, assuming that the levels of extinction and variability are negligible. Using evolutionary models, we found that a planetary-mass companion cannot produce the observed L'-band magnitude of the point-like source previously detected within the spiral arms. Our results disfavour the previously proposed protoplanet candidate MWC 758 b within the spiral arms, and our non-detection is consistent with MWC 758 c being a planetary-mass object outside the spiral arms. Sulfur chemistry in temperate N$_2$-dominated exoplanetary atmospheres investigated through experimental simulations: Volatile characterization

Louis Maratrat, Adam Yassin Jaziri, Ludovic Vettier, Audrey Chatain et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过等离子体放电实验模拟，系统研究了N₂主导的系外行星大气中硫、碳、氮元素的耦合化学反应，在SO₂和H₂S与N₂或N₂/CH₄混合物的反应中识别出包括OCS和CS₂等六种硫化物产物。通过质谱分析定量这些挥发性物种，并评估其通过凌星光谱的可观测性，发现OCS和CS₂在中红外波段具有可检测的谱线特征，可作为未来空间望远镜探测系外行星大气硫化学活动的有望靶标。
英文原摘要
Aims. This study addresses through an experimental approach an understudied aspect of sulfur atmospheric chemistry which is the coupled and complex reactivity existing between sulfur, carbon, and nitrogen elements. Experimental simulations have been used to better characterize the volatile products resulting from the coupling between these three elements and discuss the potential observability of such species. Sulfur chemistry appears as a source of diversified signatures which need to be characterized for the current and the future exploration of exoplanetary atmospheres. Methods. SO$_2$ and H$_2$S reactivity was explored experimentally in plasma discharges using mixtures of either pure N$_2$ or composed of N$_2$ and CH$_4$. The volatile species produced in such conditions were analyzed and quantified by in situ mass spectrometry. Results. SO$_2$ reactivity in the presence of N$_2$ alone or combined with methane led to the detection of six sulfur species: H$_2$S, NHSO, S$_2$O, CS$_2$, OCS, and CH$_3$SH. Under the same conditions, the reactivity of H$_2$S resulted in the observation of CS$_2$, CS, and CH$_3$SH. The detection of these species highlights the need to establish a consistent photochemical network describing this sulfur chemistry. Furthermore, the study addresses the detectability of these sulfur compounds via transit spectroscopy. Among them, OCS and CS$_2$, exhibit several detectable bands in the mid-infrared, and constitute promising targets to probe sulfur active chemistry with space- telescopes. In contrast, the detection of the other sulfur volatiles observed appears more challenging at low spectral resolution.

The Jupiter ICy moons Explorer (JUICE) Mission: Post-Launch Status and Mission Overview

Olivier Witasse, Giuseppe Sarri, Christian Erd, Philippe Gare et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
JUICE任务是ESA的大型旗舰探测器,将于2031年抵达木星系统,通过对木卫一、木卫二、木卫三和木卫四的多次近距离飞掠和环绕观测,研究这些冰卫星的内部结构、可能存在的液态水海洋及宜居性条件。任务还将综合调查木星大气的循环和能量流动、磁层三维结构及其与电离层的耦合,以及卫星与磁层间的相互作用和伽利略卫星系统的潮汐与重力演化。这项综合研究将为理解巨行星周围冰质卫星的形成、演化和宜居性提供关键观测数据,推进对类木星系统行星动力学的认识。
英文原摘要
The JUpiter ICy Moons Explorer (JUICE) is the first large-class mission in ESA's Cosmic Vision programme. Proposed in 2007 and selected in 2012, it was launched on 14 April 2023 from Kourou and is now en route towards Jupiter. After arrival in July 2031, JUICE will spend approximately four years studying the Jupiter system, performing multiple flybys of three of its Galilean moons, the icy moons Ganymede, Europa, and Callisto, before entering orbit around Ganymede for its final year. The mission will investigate conditions that may support habitable environments within the satellites, all of which are thought to contain subsurface liquid water oceans. Ganymede, the largest moon in the Solar System, is a prime target due to its internal structure and possible subsurface ocean, its unique intrinsic magnetic field, and its complex interactions with Jupiter's magnetosphere, offering a natural laboratory for studying the emergence, evolution and potential habitability of icy worlds around giant planets. JUICE will also conduct a global investigation of the Jupiter system as the archetype for gaseous giant planet systems: it will study Jupiter's atmospheric circulation, composition, and flow of energy from the cloud tops to the thermosphere and characterise the 3D structure of the magnetosphere and its coupling with the ionosphere and thermosphere. Interactions between the moons and the magnetosphere, as well as tidal and gravitational evolution within the Galilean system, will also be assessed. Additional science objectives include the innermost Galilean moon, Io and its neutral cloud and plasma torus, some small inner and irregular moons, and the ring systems. The payload includes 10 instruments and one experiment using the spacecraft's telecommunications system to connect with ground-based radio telescopes, plus an onboard radiation monitor.

The JWST Early Release Science Program for Direct Observations of Exoplanetary Systems. VIII. Patchy Forsterite and Enstatite Clouds in the Atmosphere of VHS 1256 b, Retrieval Lessons Learned and Outlook to the Future

Niall Whiteford, Jacqueline K. Faherty, Ben Burningham, Johanna M. Vos et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究采用Brewster检索框架对JWST观测的年轻行星质量伴星VHS 1256 b的1-18 μm光谱数据进行大气成分反演,通过ΔBIC方法确定其大气中存在橄榄石和顽火辉石组成的不均匀云层结构。反演结果表明该天体的云层具有斑块状覆盖特征,与其已知的显著光谱变率相符,并对H₂O、CO、CO₂、CH₄和NH₃等主要分子气体的丰度进行了约束。研究强调了检索模型对观测数据质量和不同仪器信噪比的敏感性,为后续年轻多云系外行星大气研究提供了方法学借鉴。
英文原摘要
Abstract The James Webb Space Telescope defines a new era for the data-driven approach to retrieval modeling, which has become a cornerstone tool for the statistical inference of exoplanetary and brown dwarf properties. The Early Release Science program #1386 observations of VHS 1256 b represent a huge jump in data quality, data quantity, and spectral coverage for such objects. VHS 1256 b is a young, planetary-mass and extremely variable companion that populates the enigmatic L/T cohort of substellar atmospheres. In this first retrieval analysis of the full 1–18 μ m data set, we apply the Brewster retrieval framework to the NIRSpec and MIRI spectroscopic observations of VHS 1256 b, exploring a variety of cloud species and structures. Using ΔBIC, we find that the data are best described by a forsterite (Mg 2 SiO 4 ) and enstatite (MgSiO 3 ) cloud combination. Our analysis shows a strong preference for patchy silicate cloud coverage, which aligns with VHS 1256 b’s extensive and well-documented spectral variability. Our retrieval is able to place constraints on the abundances of H 2 O, CO, CO 2 , CH 4 , as well as NH 3 . We also show that the retrieved parameters are sensitive to the data used and the relative signal-to-noise ratios between data from different instruments. We conclude with the next steps for the wider retrieval community to better understand young and cloudy exoplanetary atmospheres.

The GAPS programme at TNG. LXXXIII. Orbital refinement and atmospheric investigation of TOI-1518 b (BD+66 1610 b)

M. C. D'Arpa, F. Biassoni, F. Borsa, V. Singh et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics

TOI-1518 b (BD+66 1610 b) is a highly irradiated ultra-hot Jupiter on a strongly misaligned orbit, making it a benchmark target for atmospheric and dynamical studies. We aim to comprehensively characterise the TOI-1518 system by refining its orbital and physical parameters, and by investigating the chemical composition of the planetary atmosphere through transmission spectroscopy. We employed a joint Bayesian framework combining TESS photometry, HARPS-N high-resolution spectroscopy, and least-squares deconvolution profiles to break stellar geometrical degeneracies. We then applied single-line and cross-correlation function (CCF) transmission spectroscopy across theoretical templates. By anchoring the stellar projected rotational velocity (v i/ t ≈ 0.15 deg yr i_⋆ ≈ 79.7,̨ms), we confirmed the nearly polar orbit (łambda ≈ 238 deg) and the theoretically predicted nodal precession (mathrm d d -1 $). We revealed an atmospheric Rossiter-McLaughlin (RM) effect and a bulk atmospheric blue-shift of $≈ -7.0,̨ms. Through transmission spectroscopy, we detected ̋I, and This marks the first detection of ̋I, and in this planet, and the first ever detection of and in an exoplanetary atmosphere. Inconsistencies in the doublet shifts suggest likely stellar contamination. Furthermore, time-resolved CCF analysis revealed morning-to-evening limb asymmetries for species like and hinting at night-side cold traps. The atmosphere of TOI-1518 b exhibits a rich chemical inventory shaped by intense irradiation and bulk circulation. Our results emphasise the necessity of simultaneously modelling stellar line morphology and planetary tracks to accurately interpret atmospheric signals under strong RM effects.

Small Collisions, Big Impact: The Sensitivity of Atmospheric Temperatures to Collision-Induced Absorption

David Dahlbüdding, Tommaso Grassi, Karan Molaverdikhani, Giulia Roccetti et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究评估了碰撞诱导吸收(CIA)数据的不同来源对行星大气温度结构计算的影响。通过在理想化大气辐射转移模型中系统测试多个CIA数据源，发现当数据覆盖范围重叠时温度差异约10 K，但在谱隙或温度范围不同时可超过100 K，严重影响温度廓线收敛性。研究识别了亟需进一步实验测量和理论计算的波长和温度范围，为行星大气建模中CIA数据的选择提供了指导。
英文原摘要
Abstract Collision-induced absorption (CIA) data are relevant to determining atmospheric temperatures and, in turn, the habitability of a diverse set of planetary atmospheres. However, these data remain sparse across broad wavelength and temperature ranges. Nevertheless, multiple, sometimes disagreeing, sources of experimental and theoretical data can be available for the same CIA pair. In this paper, we assess the impact of these varying sources on atmospheric temperatures and the limitations that the available data coverage can introduce. In order to inform future measurements and calculations, we test additional cases with extrapolated wavelength and temperature ranges. We first introduce a measure of the importance of CIA opacities depending on pressure and temperature by calculating the Rosseland mean with and without each CIA pair’s contribution. Secondly, we employ each CIA source in a radiative transfer model of idealised atmospheres which maximise the effect of the CIA pair of interest. Our findings highlight the uncertainties related to the current data. Temperature differences between CIA data sources range from ∼10 K when sources overlap in wavelength and temperature coverage to &gt;100 K when spectral gaps or temperature limits differ. Spectral windows or narrow temperature coverage can also hinder the model’s convergence of the temperature profile. This work aims to provide guidance on the selection of CIA opacity datasets for atmospheric modelling and to identify the wavelength and temperature regimes in which further laboratory measurements and theoretical calculations are most urgently needed. We publish all our findings in detail in the form of an accessible website.

Prospects for detecting hot-Jupiter tidal decay through transit timing with the Earth 2.0 mission

Wenqin Wang, Yue Wang, Di-Chang Chen, Hui Zhang et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过模拟Earth 2.0卫星的高精度凌星测光观测,评估其检测热木星轨道衰减的能力。研究采用联合Kepler、TESS和模拟ET数据进行长期凌星时序变化分析,预测轨道周期变化的测量精度。结果表明ET能将热木星轨道衰减测量精度提高7.2倍,有望在三个额外的热木星系统中检测到轨道衰减,从而显著扩展轨道衰减样本量,为星-行星系统的潮汐耗散和热木星潮汐演化提供更强约束。
英文原摘要
The orbital decay of hot Jupiters provides a unique opportunity to probe tidal dissipation in star-planet systems. However, despite extensive observational efforts, only one hot Jupiter, namely, WASP-12b, currently exhibits conclusive observational evidence to support orbital decay. Thus, expanding this sample is crucial for establishing a statistical understanding of tidal interactions in planetary systems. Long-term transit timing variations (TTVs) provide a direct observational probe of orbital decay induced by tidal dissipation between a hot Jupiter and its host star. The Earth 2.0 (ET) mission will revisit the original field, delivering high-precision transit timing measurements and extending the observational baseline to approximately 24 years (2009--2033). In this work, we aim to assess the capability of ET to detect orbital decay in hot Jupiters previously observed by . Kepler Kepler We generated four years of simulated ET light curves based on the expected photometric precision and observing cadence of the mission and analyzed them jointly with archival and TESS observations. Using these combined datasets, we performed long-term TTV analyses and predicted the achievable precision for measuring orbital period variations. Kepler We find that ET can substantially improve the precision of orbital decay measurements in hot Jupiter systems by a factor of 7.2. Assuming the stellar tidal quality factor inferred from previous observations, ET is expected to detect orbital decay in three additional hot Jupiter systems. The expanded sample of hot Jupiters with measured orbital decay will establish ET as a powerful probe of tidal dissipation in star–planet systems, offering tighter constraints on stellar tidal quality factors and new insights into the tidal evolution of hot Jupiters.

Early Realignment of Eccentric Planetary Orbits without Orbital Decay

Diego J. Muñoz · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过扩展频率依赖的潮汐模型至任意偏心率，考察了恒星潮汐作用在行星轨道圆化前重新调整初始错位系统的可能性，特别关注在惯性参考系中固定的(1,0)倾斜潮汐分量。研究发现，当行星已处于偏心温木星轨道且恒星仍处于前主序阶段快速旋转时，潮汐重新调整可以超过轨道圆化速率，从而无需轨道衰减即可实现早期对齐。这一机制对解释温木星相对于热木星具有更高自旋轨道对齐度这一观测现象提供了新的解释，并对双星驱动的高偏心率迁移起源论提出了质疑。
英文原摘要
Warm Jupiters are, on average, more closely aligned with their host stars than hot Jupiters, even at high eccentricities. The low projected obliquities of eccentric warm Jupiters on the tidal migration track are difficult to reconcile with high-eccentricity migration (HEM) pathways that excite large misalignments. We examine whether tides acting on the star can realign initially misaligned systems before their orbits circularize. We extend a frequency-dependent tidal model to arbitrary eccentricities and focus on the $(1,0)$ obliquity tide. This component is stationary in the inertial frame, dissipates stellar rotational energy, and can change spin obliquity while leaving the semimajor axis and eccentricity unchanged. Its forcing frequency in the stellar rotating frame coincides with that of the rigid spin-over mode, which is non-dissipative. The strength of dissipation by non-rigid motions at this same frequency remains uncertain. In our models, enhancing the dissipative response by a factor of $10^7$ produces appreciable obliquity damping for Jupiter-mass planets. The stronger damping for more massive planets suggests that an enhancement of $10^6$ may suffice for super-Jupiters. Realignment can outpace circularization if the planet is already on an eccentric warm orbit while the star is still inflated and rapidly rotating during the pre-main sequence (PMS). If alignment occurs then, present-day stellar effective temperature may be a weak predictor of obliquity. In binary-driven HEM runs initialized at the zero-age main sequence, orbital circularization outpaces realignment in systems with sustained eccentricity forcing. The stellar quadrupole scaling suggests substantially stronger suppression of eccentricity excitation for PMS starts. These results cast doubt on binary-driven HEM as the origin of such aligned eccentric warm Jupiters, even when they lie along the tidal migration track.

Dawn of the Gaia Era: Revisiting Cold Jupiters Orbiting M Dwarfs with Astrometry+Doppler Data Joint Analysis

Yifan Xuan, Tianjun Gan, Fabo Feng, In-Gu Shin et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究联合分析地基径向速度、Hipparcos中间天体测量数据和Gaia双历元天体测量数据,对已知的绕M矮星运行的冷木星系统进行精确质量和轨道求解。在25颗行星的样本中,我们获得了13颗行星的稳健质量测定(轨道倾角不确定度<30°),包括5颗首次直接测量的行星,并将一个天体重新分类为褐矮星。比较冷木星与热木星、微引力透镜和直接成像发现的巨行星系统,我们揭示了M矮星周围可能存在高质量比热木星的不足,且本地冷木星的质量比分布与银河系核球微引力透镜样本相似的现象。
英文原摘要
We perform a joint analysis of ground-based radial velocities (RVs), Hipparcos Intermediate Astrometric Data, and two-epoch Gaia astrometry for known M dwarfs hosting cold Jupiters discovered through long-term spectroscopic monitoring. Among a sample of 25 planets in 19 systems, we derive robust absolute masses and orbital solutions for 13 planets in 11 systems with companion inclination uncertainties lower than 30 degrees. These include first mass measurements for five planets: GJ 328b, GJ 649b, GJ 849b and c, and GJ 9066c. We also reclassify one object, HIP 79431B, as a brown dwarf. We further compare the mass ratios of these 13 cold Jupiter systems with those of giant planets orbiting M stars characterized via transit, microlensing, and direct imaging. Without correcting for detection bias, we find that (1) there is likely a deficiency of high-mass-ratio hot Jupiters around M dwarfs compared with cold Jupiters, and (2) the local cold Jupiters orbiting M stars found by RV observations exhibit a similar mass ratio distribution as the counterparts in the Galactic bulge found by microlensing. Cold Jupiters orbiting stars with masses above $0.65~M_\odot$ tend to be more massive than those around M dwarfs. Our study establishes a sample of local cold Jupiters with precise mass determinations around M dwarfs, and reveal a tip of the iceberg in the upcoming Gaia DR4 and DR5.

A Deep Learning Framework for Transit Timing Variation Characterization: A Case Study of the Kepler-88 System

Chen Chen, Jiapeng Gao, Gongjie Li, Lingkai Kong et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究设计了一个基于Transformer架构的深度学习框架，用于从凌日时间变化（TTV）和凌日时长变化（TDV）信息反演非凌日伴星行星的质量和轨道参数，相比传统MCMC方法在单行星凌日系统中具有优势。以Kepler-88系统为例，该模型在半长轴、离心率上达到≲10%的分数误差，在行星质量上达到10%−30%的精度，在TTV半振幅为0.3秒至5分钟范围内的测试集上表现出更优性能。该数据驱动方法为复杂多行星系统的参数反演提供了新的思路，但仍需扩展至更广泛的行星构型和多伴星系统。
英文原摘要
Abstract Transit timing variation (TTV) provides rich information about the mass and orbital properties of exoplanets, which are often obtained by solving an inverse problem via Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). In this paper, we design a new data-driven approach that potentially can be applied to problems that are hard for traditional MCMC methods, such as the case with only one planet transiting. Specifically, we use a deep learning approach to predict the parameters of a nontransit companion for the single transit system with transit information (i.e., TTV and transit duration variation (TDV)) as input. This approach is constructed using the Transformer -based architecture that can extract long-range interactions from TTV sequential data. We focus on two-planet systems based on Kepler-88 as a starting point, with the inner planet fixed to the properties of Kepler-88 and the mass and orbital elements of the outer planet varied. Applying to Kepler-88, we obtained an overall fractional error of ≲10% on semimajor axis and eccentricity, and ∼10%−30% on planet mass (assuming that the stellar and transiting planet parameters are known), based on a training set of a million simulations. When applied to the test set, our model achieves low fractional errors of less than a few percent for systems with TTV semiamplitudes ranging from ∼0.3 s to ∼5 minutes. Extending our approach to a broader range of planetary architectures, including systems with substantially weaker or stronger TTV signals and those containing additional planets or stellar companions, remains an important direction for future work.

The long-period central star of the planetary nebula MPAJ1508-6455

Adam Mousley, David Jones, Matti Dorsch, Rajeev Manick et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过长期高分辨率光谱监测测定了行星状星云MPAJ1508-6455中心双星的径向速度曲线和大气参数,并结合测光数据和星云的运动学分析,确定其轨道周期超过五年且离心率很高。该系统由低温sdO型中心星和K型伴星组成,其中心星的Kiel质量与径向速度导出的动力学质量存在显著差异,而星云的丰度模式支持较低质量的前身星且不经历热底部燃烧,更加一致于Kiel质量。这是已知仅有的第四个轨道周期超过一年的行星状星云中心双星系统。
英文原摘要
A significant fraction of planetary nebulae have been shown to host short-period binary central stars, but longer-period central stars are scarcer. We characterise the central star of the planetary nebula MPAJ1508-6455, which has previously been claimed to be an intermediate-period binary with a period of approximately 12 days. We studied archival and catalogue photometry to probe the previously reported photometric variability and binary spectral energy distribution. We used long-term high-resolution spectroscopic monitoring to measure the radial velocity curves of the two components of the central binary, and to derive their atmospheric parameters. We also undertook kinematical and chemical abundance analyses of the nebula itself. The orbital period of the central binary of exceeds five years and is highly eccentric. The binary comprises a relatively low-temperature sdO-type central star and a spun-up K-type giant companion. The Kiel mass of the central star is approximately half the dynamical mass derived from the radial velocities. While the systemic velocity and inclination of the nebula are consistent with the radial velocities of the binary, its abundance pattern adds support for a lower-mass progenitor that did not experience hot bottom burning (and is thus more consistent with the Kiel mass than the mass derived from the radial velocities). To the best of our knowledge, the central star of is only the fourth planetary nebula central star to have a spectroscopically confirmed orbital period longer than one year.

Shifting the snow line in protoplanetary disks by planet-induced multiple spiral shocks

Shizu Shimizu, Ryosuke T. Tominaga, Satoshi Okuzumi, Takayuki Muto · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过二维流体力学模拟和热平衡计算,量化了巨行星诱导的多重螺旋激波对原行星盘雪线位置的影响。研究发现,当行星质量和盘光学厚度足够大时,激波加热可显著提高盘内部温度,例如质量为木星三分之一、轨道半径5 au的行星可将雪线外推至1 au外。这一结果表明原始木星的早期形成可能通过激波加热为陆地行星在日光层内部的原位形成提供了必要的热源条件。
英文原摘要
The snow line location provides important constraints on rocky planet formation. Recent magnetohydrodynamical simulations suggest that Joule heating associated with gas accretion is so inefficient that the snow line can be located inside 1 au around a solar-type star. This challenges in-situ formation of the terrestrial planets in the solar system unless other internal heating processes are present. Previous studies have proposed that spiral shocks induced by an embedded planet potentially shift the snow line outward. In this study, we investigate the impact of shock heating induced by a giant planet on the location of the snow line in two steps. First, we perform two-dimensional hydrodynamical simulations of a disk with a giant planet to model shock heating rates. We construct an empirical formula for total entropy jumps due to multiple spiral shocks interior to the planetary orbit. Second, we calculate the radial temperature profile based on the thermal balance with the modeled shock heating rates. We find that shock heating can substantially increase the disk temperature in inner regions far from the planet when the planetary mass and disk optical depth are sufficiently high. For example, assuming a fixed surface density profile and an opacity of $κ= 1~\rm cm^2\,g^{-1}$, a planet with one-third of Jupiter's current mass at 5 au can shift the snow line outward beyond 1 au. Our results suggest that the early formation of proto-Jupiter in the solar nebula may have enabled rocky planetesimals and protoplanets to form in the present-day terrestrial planet region without invoking additional water removal processes.

Probabilistic, facility-specific prioritisation of temperate exoplanets for atmospheric characterisation with JWST and the ELT

英文原摘要
Deciding which temperate exoplanets to observe first matters for JWST transit spectroscopy and for planned ELT reflected-light instruments, yet signal-to-noise metrics ignore habitability and similarity indices ignore observability. We present an open Monte Carlo framework (UHI v6.1) that builds probabilistic priority lists separately for each facility and composition class, never as a single ranking. For each of 6,112 planets in the NASA Exoplanet Archive, 10,000 draws carry catalogue uncertainties through climate, cumulative stellar-XUV atmospheric retention, per-draw rocky, Hycean or gas-rich classes, and a JWST (transiting) or ELT/PCS (non-transiting) detection model, giving each planet a survival probability, a conditional score and a top-N probability. At $5σ$, 255 rocky and 71 Hycean candidates remain viable. A Saltelli--Sobol analysis of 32 assumptions ($N = 512$) shows that the location and width of the XUV shoreline dominate the rocky lists, with total-order indices of 0.22 and 0.12 at $5σ$ (0.25 and 0.14 at $3σ$), and all ten leading indices have 95% bootstrap half-widths below 0.05. For Hycean planets most outputs are degenerate and the analysis does not converge, so that branch is reported as indicative. Membership of these lists is limited mainly by whether a planet survives the habitability vetoes: at $5σ$, 3 of the nominal JWST and 4 of the nominal ELT rocky top-10 targets enter the top 10 in at least half of all draws, yet every one of them stays in the top 10 in more than half of the draws in which it survives. Code, per-planet posterior summaries, sensitivity tables and maps are public, so observers can rerun the lists under their own instrument assumptions and see which inputs would most change a target's priority.

英文原摘要
Terraforming Mars is a long-horizon, action-conditioned control problem that requires predicting atmospheric responses to interventions over centuries to identify effective policies. This position paper argues that physics-regularized recurrent state-space world models are the most promising foundation for this setting. Data-driven climate emulators lack action-conditioning, and physics-based general circulation models (GCMs) are too expensive for policy search. Among learned world models, recurrent state-space architectures with physics constraints are better suited than transformer-based alternatives: they provide native uncertainty quantification through their stochastic-deterministic decomposition, remain stable under sparse non-terrestrial training data, and enforce physical consistency at each rollout step. To make this position concrete, we describe a potential architecture combining JEPA-inspired latent encoding, universal differential equation constraints, and a Dreamer-lineage planning head. We also introduce MarsWorld, an open benchmark proposal for intervention-conditioned planetary world models, and discuss broader implications and objections.

Abstract The detection of Earth analogs with extreme-precision radial velocities is limited by spectral variability from stellar activity, telluric absorption, and instrumental systematics. We apply Æstra, a generative spectrum modeling framework, to NEID Sun-as-a-star observations. Æstra empirically decomposes the spectra into stellar line-shape variability, microtelluric absorption, and continuum variability without external atmospheric or stellar templates. Estimations for strong and weak hot Jupiter magnetism. A Python package with interpolators and scaling laws

Albert Elias-López, Aline Vidotto, Daniele Viganò, J. R. Fuentes et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过一维演化内部模型结合磁场标度律，在深加热和浅加热两种情景下估算了热木星的磁场强度范围（强场~100 G，弱场~1 G）。研究开发了基于观测量（行星质量、半径和入射恒星通量）的易用标度律关系，并提供了Python插值工具包用于预测现有热木星种群的磁场。这些磁场估算为解释未来大气风速测量、星-行星相互作用信号和射电观测提供了理论框架，有助于区分不同的行星内部加热机制。
英文原摘要
Hot Jupiters (HJs) are commonly thought to host the strongest dynamo-generated magnetic fields among exoplanets, exceeding Jupiter's by up to an order of magnitude, which can potentially induce observable star--planet interaction (SPI) and planetary radio emission. However, while several indirect, model-dependent estimates of HJ magnetic fields have been reported from SPIs and recent transmission spectroscopy diagnostics, no unambiguous direct (radio) detection has yet been achieved. We aim to provide a range of magnetic field estimates for HJs, considering at the same time the mechanisms responsible for inflation and the convective flux that enters into the magnetic field scaling law. Given that their dynamos operate in the fast-rotating regime (Rossby number łesssim 0.1), we combined one-dimensional evolutionary interior models with magnetic field scaling laws under two limiting heating scenarios within the convective zone: deep and shallow energy depositions. Using interpolators built from the simulated data output, we provide magnetic field predictions for the HJ population. The deep-heating scenario maximizes convective heat flux in the dynamo and predicts strong magnetic fields of up to ∼100 G, whereas shallow heating, which is arguably more realistic for most proposed physical mechanisms, weakens convection and yields much weaker fields of $ ̧al O (1)$ G. The strong field scenario is compatible with indirect SPI detections, and the weak field scenario is in agreement with recent atmospheric constraints. We derived easy-to-use scaling law relations based on observables, i.e., planetary mass, radius, and/or incident stellar flux, that roughly reproduce the magnetic field estimates of our interior models: B_ ̊m strong = 19 , ̊m P ^ 0.6 R_ ̊m P ^ 1.7 G and B_ ̊m weak = 0.8 , M_ ̊m P ^ 1.5 R_ ̊m P ^ -1.2 G. With this range of magnetic field estimates for the current observed HJ population, our predictions provide a framework for interpreting future atmospheric wind measurements, SPI signatures, and radio observations, which may ultimately discriminate between competing interior-heating scenarios.

M Dwarf Singles and Multis Have Dissimilar Metallicities Indicating Different Origins

Aida Behmard, Sheila Sagear, Yuxi(Lucy) Lu, Romy Rodríguez Martínez et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过比较M矮星单行星系统和多行星系统的宿主星金属丰度，发现M矮星多行星系统显著贫金属（p=0.0008），且金属丰度随行星数量增加而递减，而单行星系统中的行星具有更高离心率和更短轨道周期。研究解释这些差异反映了M矮星单多系统源于不同的动力学过程，其中金属丰度通过影响原行星盘性质进而影响行星系统的动力学演化，而在更大质量的FGK矮星原行星盘中可能不存在这种明显的金属丰度依赖性。
英文原摘要
Abstract It is unclear if host star metallicities differ between systems with one detected transiting planet (“singles”) or multiple detected transiting planets (“multis”) around FGK dwarfs. Here, we find strong evidence that they do for M dwarfs. We use a homogeneous sample of FGK and M dwarf chemical abundances to demonstrate that M dwarf multis are significantly more metal-poor than singles ( p = 0.0008), even when we only consider small (<4 R ⊕ ) planets ( p = 0.001). This result emerges at the late-K to M dwarf transition. We observe that M dwarf multis become more metal-poor as a function of increasing planet multiplicity. Additionally, planets in singles have significantly higher eccentricities and shorter orbital periods compared to planets in multis. We interpret these findings as evidence that M dwarf singles lead relatively active dynamical lives, and the different planetary architectures of singles and multis arise from distinct dynamics influenced by metallicity. This may not occur in FGK systems because their more massive protoplanetary disks may supply enough planet-forming material to instigate dynamics that lead to only one transiting planet, even when the host star metallicity is low. Another possibility is that multis are less compact around FGK dwarfs because they form from larger disks, which may often cause them to be observed as singles in transit data.

英文原摘要
Abstract We present high-angular-resolution ( ∼ 0 . ″ 1 − 0 . ″ 5 ) Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) observations of millimeter-wave line and continuum emission (at ∼0.44 and 0.88 mm) in the preplanetary nebula IRAS 06530–0213 (IRAS 06530). These data show the presence of an extended circular ring—first evidence of the last thermal pulse preceding the post-asymptotic giant branch (AGB) phase in a carbon star—and a central bipolar nebula (CBN) and torus. The mass-loss rate of IRAS 06530’s AGB progenitor decreased immediately after the thermal pulse, then rose again just before the post-AGB phase, as evidenced by the presence of filamentary arc structures around the CBN. The arc stuctures are likely part of a 3D Archimedean spiral structure generally attributed to the presence of a binary companion. However, we do not find a compact continuum source at the location of IRAS 06530’s central star, such as that associated with the compact dusty disks typically found in disk-prominent post-AGB objects known to have binary companions. The expansion ages derived for the torus and CBN imply that IRAS 06530’s progenitor transitioned to a post-AGB star ≲700 yr ago. The molecular mass of the ejecta in IRAS 06530 is dominated by the filamentary arc region, with a mass M H 2 = ( 0.12 − 0.25 ) M ⊙ . Compared with solar values, the torus of IRAS 06530 appears to be significantly (modestly) enriched in 13 C and 17 O ( 15 N), as well—a pattern of rare-isotope enrichment inconsistent with standard nucleosynthesis models. From the luminosity of IRAS 06530 and the age of its detached shell, while it was still on the AGB, we infer that the mass of IRAS 06530’s progenitor was 1.6–3.4 M ⊙ .

First Detection of the Forbidden Rotational Spectrum of H 3 + : JWST/MIRI Observations of the Shocked Gas in HH 168

David A. Neufeld, Joel D. Green, Dan M. Watson, Paule Sonnentrucker · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究利用JWST/MIRI首次在天体观测中探测到H₃⁺离子的禁戒旋转谱线，在HH 168激波区域检测到26条谱线，其旋转温度约730 K但不符合热分布。通过与J型和C型激波模型对比，发现J型激波的H₃⁺发射预测与观测较为吻合（误差因子~1.5），而C型激波预测不足5-6倍，表明高能碰撞激发的物理过程仍需深入理解。该研究揭示了H₃⁺禁戒旋转谱作为激波区电离状态新诊断工具的潜力，对行星大气和星际离子-分子化学研究具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
Abstract The H 3 + molecular ion is the cornerstone of interstellar ion-molecule chemistry. Its vibrational transitions have long been observed, in absorption along many interstellar sight lines and in emission from the upper atmospheres of the giant planets, but its pure rotational spectrum, which consists only of “forbidden” transitions made possible by centrifugal distortion, had not been observed in the laboratory or in any astronomical source. With JWST’s Mid-Infrared Instrument (MIRI), we have detected this spectrum toward the Herbig-Haro object HH 168 in Cepheus A West. Twenty-six lines are detected above 5 σ in one or both of two fields, of which the ( J , K ) = (5, 0) → (4, 3) line at 16.325 μ m is the strongest. The H 3 + lines share the velocity and spatial distribution of the hot shocked H 2 , not of the ionized gas. A rotation diagram yields a rotational temperature of ≈730 K, but the level populations are not thermal and are best explained by subthermal excitation in gas at ≳2000 K. We compare the observations with models of shocks propagating into mildly irradiated molecular gas. Constrained by the H 2 rotational lines, a dissociative J-type shock predicts H 3 + emission within a factor ∼1.5 of that observed, while a nondissociative C-type shock, which fits the H 2 lines better, underpredicts it by a factor 5–6, although the excitation of H 3 + by energetic collisions is poorly known. Archival MIRI data show the H 3 + forbidden rotational spectrum in the HH 211 bow shock. The H 3 + lines provide a new probe of the ionization in shocked molecular gas.

Sulfur photochemistry traces mantle redox states of rocky planets

Ioannis Panagiotou, Tim Lichtenberg, Shang-Min Tsai, Harrison Nicholls · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过耦合行星内部-大气演化模型与化学动力学模型，系统调查了岩石系外行星岩浆洋阶段的硫化物光化学过程如何受地幔氧化还原状态的控制。研究发现，大气主要成分由地幔氧化态和出气历史决定，而光化学过程改变上层大气的中性挥发物丰度并增强自由基，使得硫物种在中等到富氧的条件下产生可被JWST探测的特征吸收谱线。这些研究结果表明硫物种可作为追踪系外行星内部氧化还原状态的有效示踪剂，为通过光谱观测深入了解行星内部结构提供了新途径。
英文原摘要
Volatile outgassing from planetary interiors controls the composition of rocky exoplanets' secondary atmospheres. However, observations indicate that disequilibrium processes can strongly alter the chemical structure of hot Jupiters. How outgassing and photochemistry jointly determine the resulting atmospheric composition remains an open question. Sulfur-bearing species are promising tracers of interior-atmosphere coupling because their atmospheric abundances are sensitive to both mantle redox state and stellar irradiation. We investigate how photochemistry modifies outgassed atmospheres of diverse Earth-sized worlds during the magma ocean phase and assess how sulfur traces the interior oxidation state. The planetary interior-atmosphere evolution modelling framework is coupled to two chemical models, and . We ran a grid of planetary evolution simulations spanning diverse mantle redox states, instellation fluxes, and host-star spectra. For each case, we compared atmospheric compositions with three post-processing calculations: thermochemical equilibrium, with disequilibrium vertical transport and with vertical transport plus photochemistry. PROTEUS FastChem VULCAN The bulk atmospheric composition remains controlled by the mantle redox state and the history of outgassing. Reduced mantles produce atmospheres rich in and and oxidised mantles are dominated by and Photochemistry affects the low-pressure upper atmosphere, strongly depleting neutral volatiles and enhancing radicals. is strongly enhanced at intermediate-to-oxidised redox states. Synthetic emission spectra show that photochemical can generate absorption features at 4,μm and at 7.3,/,8.7,μm, reaching and respectively, before sequentially returning to the outgassed signatures of and for the oxidised mantle redox state. These signatures are potentially detectable with JWST, motivating targeted observational campaigns. Sulfur species link mantle redox state, volcanic outgassing, and atmospheric disequilibrium, acting as promising probes of exoplanets' interiors for spectroscopic observations.

散射与动力学演化2 篇

Lower-Mass Giants Can Spare Inner Planets during High-Eccentricity Migration

Qunfeng Jiang · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过耗散三体数值积分系统研究了不同质量巨行星在高偏心率迁移过程中对内侧行星的动力学影响，发现低于木星质量的巨行星能够减弱对内轨道的扰动，同时缩短潮汐圆化时间，从而在动力学稳定性和潮汐演化之间形成可行窗口。结合已知的伴星系统验证，该研究表明次木星质量巨行星可以通过高偏心率迁移机制形成接近恒星的轨道，同时保留内侧伴星，为这一行星形成通道提供了新的正面证据。
英文原摘要
The survival of an inner planet next to a close-in giant is commonly used to argue against high-eccentricity migration (HEM), because the eccentric giant is expected to destabilize the inner system. Existing survival calculations have concentrated on Jupiter-mass migrants, although several Jupiter-sized close-in giants with nearby companions have sub-Jovian masses. We use dissipative three-body integrations to measure the collision-survival boundary at seven giant masses from $0.25$ to $1.0,M_{\rm Jup}$ and combine it with uncompressed equilibrium-tide circularization times. At fixed physical periapse, lower giant mass weakens the forcing of the inner orbit and shifts the survival boundary inward. It also shortens the circularization time for a common radius and tidal prescription. These dynamical and tidal limits enclose a viable HEM region in which a sub-Jovian giant can circularize within a system age while retaining an ultra-short-period companion. We screen known companion-hosting close-in giants and perform system-specific integrations of WASP-132; every current candidate lies outside the region. A future system inside it would show directly that HEM can produce a close-in giant while preserving an inner planet, converting companion survival into positive evidence for this formation channel.

Where Did the Angular Momentum Go? WASP-12 b May Have Circularized on a Retrograde Orbit

Elina Y. Zhang, Fei Dai, Yaguang Li, Jennifer van Saders et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
WASP-12 b是唯一确认轨道衰减的系外行星,但其衰减轨道转移的角动量应该重新调整主星的对流层,却观测到约60°的自转-轨道不对齐,这提出了角动量去向的问题。本研究通过新的Rossiter-McLaughlin测量和演化模型分析表明,WASP-12系统初期处于逆行轨道状态,潮汐衰减过程中轨道角动量转移到主星对流包层使其表面与轨道对齐,而逆行内部结构作为角动量储库导致表面自转减缓,其内-外层角动量交换时标与观测的轨道衰减时标相符。该结果提出了一个新的高偏心率迁移机制,解释了行星轨道衰减时角动量的重新分配过程。
英文原摘要
WASP-12 b is the only exoplanet with confirmed orbital decay. Angular momentum transferred from its decaying orbit should have realigned the star's thin convective layer, yet an earlier Rossiter--McLaughlin (RM) measurement suggested a $\sim$60$^\circ$ spin--orbit misalignment. We present two new Keck Planet Finder RM measurements. We measure a sky-projected obliquity of $λ=-1.3\pm8.7^\circ$; and the star's projected rotation velocity is $v\sin i_\star=1.3\pm0.1~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, an order of magnitude lower than expected for a star of its mass and age. With WASP-12's low chromospheric activity, $\log R^\prime_{\rm HK}=-5.5$, our analysis favors an aligned system in which $v\sin i_\star$ reflects slow surface rotation with $P_{\rm rot}\sim60$ days, rather than a projection effect. Our evolution models favor a late-main-sequence star rather than a subgiant, with a central convective core found in 99\% of posterior samples. This internal structure suppresses the central-focusing and nonlinear wave-breaking mechanisms for inward-propagating gravity waves. We propose that WASP-12 b was initially placed on a retrograde orbit after high-eccentricity migration, when its host rotated at $v\sin i_\star\approx14$~km~s$^{-1}$ (or $P_{\rm rot}\sim$6 days). Tidal decay transferred orbital angular momentum to the convective envelope, aligning the surface. The retrograde interior acts as an angular-momentum reservoir, while interior--envelope coupling slows the surface rotation. A semi-empirical relation gives an envelope--interior angular-momentum exchange timescale of $\sim4$~Myr, similar to the observed orbital-decay timescale of $\sim3.25$~Myr. The angular-momentum budget also permits this initially retrograde evolution. In contrast, an initially prograde planet would have spun up the surface rotation to a rate substantially higher than observed.

On the High Eccentricity Orbit of 51 Eri b and Constraints on Interactions with a Second Companion

William O. Balmer, William Thompson, Rodrigo P. Silva, Laurent Pueyo et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究基于VLTI/GRAVITY五年高精度光学干涉测量数据，更新了年轻行星51 Eri b的轨道参数，确认其轨道离心率极高(e=0.574±0.003)。通过分析主星的位置残差，研究发现可能存在第二伴星，动力学模型表明该伴星质量和半长轴范围分别为1.0-2900木星质量和0.3-3.1天文单位，两行星间的动力学相互作用可能将外层行星b抛向现有的高离心轨道。该研究为理解年轻多行星系统中动力学散射过程提供了关键观测证据。
英文原摘要
The eccentricity of an exoplanet's orbit is a key observational quantity that can be used to infer its dynamical past and predict its future orbital evolution. Planets are expected to emerge from their protoplanetary cradles with roughly circular eccentricities, and non-circular eccentricites to arise from subsequent dynamical processing in the absence of the gas disk. We present an updated orbit for 51 Eridani b based on 5 years of 0.2 mas precision optical interferometry from VLTI/GRAVITY(+). This planet is young (${\sim}20\,\mathrm{Myr}$ old), temperate ($T_{\rm eff}\sim650\,\mathrm{K}$), and on a wide orbit ($a{=}9.36\,\mathrm{au}$) with an uncertain mass ($M_{\rm b}\leq12\,M_{\rm J}$). We measured the planet's motion to an exceptionally high precision, confirming that its orbit is very eccentric ($e_b=0.574\pm0.003$). We observe residuals to the absolute astrometry of the host star based on a single planet orbit that could be attributable to reflex motion from a third body in the system. We construct a two companion orbit model that could explain the apparent reflex motion of 51 Eri A: this model predicts that the putative second companion has $M_c\in[1.0, 2900]_{95\%}\,M_{\rm Jup}$ and $a_c\in[0.3, 3.1]_{95\%}\,\mathrm{au}$. In this framework, a dynamical interaction between b and ``c" could have kicked the exterior, lower mass b out onto its present, eccentric orbit. If this signal is astrophysical, and not due to a systematic error, this candidate should be confirmable with Gaia DR4 epoch astrometry in the near future.
