Validation and Physical Characterization of Seven TESS Exoplanets: A Compact Multi-Planet System and Targets for Atmospheric Spectroscopy

Biel Escolà-Rodrigo · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用TRICERATOPS引擎和贝叶斯推断框架对TESS发现的七颗系外行星候选体进行了统计验证和物理表征,通过自动信号处理、像素级质心分析和假阳性概率计算,成功验证了六颗新系外行星。研究确定了TOI-2392三行星系统的周期比关系,并识别出TOI-7721.01和TOI-1277.02具有最高的透射光谱指标,是JWST和Ariel后续大气观测的优先目标。
英文原摘要
The Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) continues to identify thousands of transit candidates that require statistical validation to rule out astrophysical false positives before atmospheric characterization can be pursued. We statistically validate and characterize seven TESS exoplanet candidates orbiting five host stars. As a consistency check of our automated vetting and Bayesian inference pipeline, we include TOI-2392.03, a sub-Neptune companion previously validated in the literature. Our framework combines automated signal retrieval, pixel-level centroid and Gaia DR3 contamination analysis, and false positive probability (FPP) computation with the TRICERATOPS vetting engine. Physical and orbital parameters are derived via nested sampling (juliet/Dynesty), complemented by an empirical mass-radius relation, since no dynamical mass measurements are available. Applying the same procedure to all candidates, we statistically validate six new exoplanets ($\mathrm{FPP} < 0.015$ and $\mathrm{NFPP} < 0.001$), one of which (TOI-7721.01) lies close to the FPP threshold. We report the period ratios of the three-planet system TOI-2392, and identify TOI-7721.01 and TOI-1277.02 as the candidates with the highest Transmission Spectroscopy Metrics (TSM) in our sample, making them targets of interest for atmospheric follow-up with the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) and the upcoming Ariel mission.
transiting exoplanet · planet candidate

Detection and Statistics of Large-scale Sector Boundary Crossings in the Solar Wind: The SBCross Catalog

Stephan G. Heinemann, Manuela Temmer, Mathew Owens, Emilia Kilpua et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过联合行星际磁场和超热电子俯仰角分布数据,建立了包含1076个扇区边界穿越事件的SBCross目录(覆盖1995-2026年三个太阳周期),自动化识别日球流动层的大尺度极性转变结构。研究发现扇区边界穿越平均每个卡林顿自转周期出现2.6次,中位持续时间约9.0小时,物理厚度约7.4×10⁶千米,其中约15%的事件与行星际日冕物质抛射相互作用,这些CME相关事件表现出更长的持续时间、更大的厚度和更高的太阳风速度,特别是所有超600 km/s的高速事件均由CME相互作用驱动。该目录为日球物理研究提供了系统研究扇区边界转变过程中的太阳风动力学和空间天气影响的标准化公开资源。
英文原摘要
Abstract Heliospheric sector boundary crossings (SBCs), composed of the heliospheric current sheet (HCS), heliospheric plasma sheet (HPS), and surrounding plasma halo, represent fundamental structures in the solar wind that influence interplanetary dynamics and space weather. We present the SBCross catalog, an automated, publicly available database containing 1076 SBC events derived from in situ observations spanning more than 30 yr (1995–2026), covering Solar Cycles 23 and 24 alongside the rising and maximum phases of Solar Cycle 25. By combining interplanetary magnetic field (IMF) and 255 eV suprathermal electron pitch angle distribution (PAD) data, our algorithm reliably captures large-scale polarity transitions, with 76% of detected SBCs exhibiting clear HPS signatures ( β > 1, n peak > 10 cm −3 ). Across the catalog, we find an average occurrence rate of ≈2.6 events per Carrington rotation, a median event duration of ≈9.0 hr, and a median physical thickness of ≈7.4 × 10 6 km. Furthermore, approximately ≈15% of detected SBCs interact with interplanetary coronal mass ejections (CMEs), with this proportion rising up to 33% during solar maximum. These CME-associated events exhibit systematically longer durations (median of 10.0 hr), 54% larger physical thicknesses, and higher average solar wind velocities. Notably, all high-velocity SBC events (>600 km s −1 ) are exclusively driven by CME interactions, demonstrating local plasma pileup and structural entanglement resulting from fast CMEs overtaking the ambient slow background. The SBCross catalog serves as an updated successor to the classic SBC list by L. Svalgaard, delivering a standardized, open-access resource for the heliophysics community to systematically investigate sector boundary transitions.
occurrence rate

Improving the Precision of Line-by-line Radial Velocities: A Data-driven Iterative Algorithm for Spectral Line Selection

Kanishk Pandey, Eric Ford, Joseph M. Salzer, Jessi Cisewski-Kehe et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过逐线分析(LBL)方法测量了383天NEID太阳观测中3830条谱线的径向速度,并开发了FLARES迭代算法,基于谱线深度、信噪比、探测器位置等多个指标自动筛选最优谱线子集。研究表明,精心选择的较小谱线列表相比使用全部谱线或标准方法能显著改进径向速度精度,FLARES算法仅用25-100条谱线即可将RV均方根误差降至~1.3 m/s,优于现有基准方法,为未来极高精度系外行星探测奠定了基础。
英文原摘要
Abstract Independent analysis of individual spectral lines, or line-by-line (LBL) analyses, can improve upon standard cross-correlation function (CCF) methods for measuring radial velocities (RVs) because they preserve critical information about individual line-shape changes that can be caused by stellar activity. In this work, we measure LBL RVs of 3830 spectral lines across 383 days of NEID solar observations. Our LBL approach achieves an RV rms of 2.012 m s −1 using all lines. Then, we describe several benchmark methods to select smaller line lists based on line properties such as depth and intrinsic RV scatter. Our primary finding is that a smaller line list can result in higher-quality and more stable RVs than standard RV extraction approaches. Motivated by these results, we present Filtering Lines for Accurate Radial-velocity Exoplanet Search (FLARES), an iterative line-selection algorithm. FLARES selects candidate spectral lines with extreme values of multiple line metrics and properties such as depth, signal-to-noise ratio, and detector position, and preferentially rejects lines whose removal produces the largest decrease in the weighted RV scatter. We find that FLARES-selected line lists can substantially reduce the RV rms with substantially fewer lines, outperforming the full line list and benchmark line-selection methods. Cross-validation tests demonstrate that FLARES is robust, yielding an RV rms of ≈1.3 m s −1 using just ≈25−100 lines. Comparisons to alternative line lists with properties similar to the best FLARES-selected lines demonstrate that FLARES successfully identifies line properties that lead to effective line lists for future extreme-precision radial velocity measurements.
radial velocity

Atmospheric constraints and transit-timing variations of HAT-P-36 b over 14 years

Ida A Janiak, Eamonn Kerins, Supachai Awiphan, Napaporn A-thano et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究基于14年间149条凌星光变曲线(包括47条新的地基观测)对热木星HAT-P-36b进行了系统的光度学和凌星时序分析,结合TESS和VarAstro/ETD数据获得迄今最长的时间基线。通过联合建模和大气检索,我们精确测定了行星参数、改进了星历预报,并从波长相关的半径比推断出μ = 2.22 ± 0.38的分子量,指示存在高空霾层的氢氦主导大气。凌星时序分析发现一个周期~61.3天、振幅2.40 ± 0.38分钟的周期信号,其起源可能来自恒星活动或系统内存在的共振行星伴星。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present a photometric and transit-timing analysis of the hot Jupiter HAT-P-36,b based on 149 transit light curves spanning 14 years, including 47 newly acquired, previously unpublished ground-based observations. These data are combined with photometry from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite and the VarAstro/ETD catalogue, providing the longest temporal baseline for this system to date. Using a joint modelling approach, we refine the planetary parameters and derive an updated ephemeris. From the wavelength dependence of the planet-to-star radius ratio, we investigate atmospheric scattering and measure a mean molecular weight of μ = 2.22 ± 0.38, consistent with a hydrogen–helium dominated atmosphere and favouring the presence of high-altitude hazes. We further constrain the atmospheric properties through a broadband atmospheric retrieval. No robust evidence for secular orbital period evolution is found after accounting for the heterogeneous timing uncertainties. A transit-timing analysis reveals a periodic signal with a period of ∼61.3 days and an amplitude of 2.40 ± 0.38 minutes. The origin of this signal remains uncertain and may be attributed to stellar activity or the presence of an additional planetary companion near a mean-motion resonance.

行星形成与盘演化2 篇

Dependence of Dust Growth on Magnetic field Strength in Protoplanetary Disks

Takashi Shimokawa, Masahiro Machida, Yoshihiro Kawasaki · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过一维计算同时演化尘埃粒度分布和垂直磁场，自洽地估算MRI驱动的湍流粘度，并考虑尘埃碰撞聚并、破碎以及非理想磁流体效应，调查磁流体动力学不稳定性对原行星盘中尘埃生长的影响。结果表明MRI驱动的湍流在低初始等离子体贝塔值时增强，促进MRI活跃区的尘埃生长，但尘埃生长最终受径向漂移限制，磁场强度对尘埃粒度的影响仅在盘外区域显著。研究揭示在原行星盘大部分区域，径向漂移而非磁场强度是控制尘埃最大粒度的主导因素。
英文原摘要
We performed one-dimensional calculations to investigate how the magnetorotational instability (MRI) affects dust growth in protoplanetary disks. We simultaneously solved the evolution of the dust size distribution and the vertical magnetic field. The dust size distribution is evolved taking into ac- count coagulation and fragmentation. For the magnetic field evolution, we considered both ambipolar diffusion and Ohmic dissipation. The MRI-driven turbulent viscosity was estimated self-consistently from the local magnetic field strength and non-ideal magnetohydrodynamics diffusivities. We found that MRI-driven turbulence becomes stronger for smaller initial plasma beta, and the enhanced tur- bulence promotes dust growth in MRI-active regions. However, dust growth is ultimately limited by radial drift, and the dependence of dust size on the magnetic field strength is significant only in the outer regions of the disk. In other words, over most of the disk, radial drift remains the dominant factor controlling the maximum dust size, and the magnetic field strength plays only a minor role in determining the overall dust size distribution.
protoplanetary disk

The Life and Death of Stars that Capture Primordial Black Holes

Ore Gottlieb, Matteo Cantiello, Cameron Norton, Ken Van Tilburg et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究建立了捕获原始黑洞的恒星演化的完整框架，结合解析计算、恒星演化模型、三维相对论磁流体动力学模拟和蒙特卡洛种群合成，揭示这类系统的演化分为两个分支：若黑洞在消耗主星前形成吸积盘则导致爆炸式破坏，否则安静消耗主星。研究发现三体相互作用是黑洞捕获的主导机制，黑洞的最终质量为0.01–1太阳质量且自旋参数a*≈0.8，其产生的瞬变事件可能对应低光度伽玛射线暴，为原始黑洞暗物质探测和亚太阳质量黑洞并合源提供了新途径。
英文原摘要
Abstract Primordial black holes (PBHs) in the asteroid mass window (10 17 –10 23 g) remain viable dark matter candidates and can be captured by stars. We develop the first global framework for the evolution of stars that capture PBHs, combining analytic calculations, stellar evolution models, 3D general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations, and Monte Carlo population synthesis. We find that the fate of these systems bifurcates: PBHs that form an accretion disk before consuming the host drive explosive disruption, whereas PBHs captured too late or growing too slowly consume the star quietly. Capture is dominated by three-body interactions with planetary or stellar companions. For a solar-type host with a Jupiter analog, inspiral within a main-sequence lifetime requires M BH crit ≳ 1 0 22 g , while lighter PBHs generally require tighter companions. Once deposited at the center, the PBH grows through inefficient quasi-spherical Bondi accretion; if it reaches the angular-momentum threshold before consuming the host, the inflow circularizes into a disk. Our Monte Carlo calculations yield sizable quiet-consumption and explosive-disruption populations, with final PBH masses M BH ∼ 0.01–1 M ⊙ and disk-forming PBH spins a * ≈ 0.8. Disk formation is the point of no return: disk winds and relativistic jets of ∼10 45 –10 50 erg s −1 disrupt the star within minutes. The resulting transients may include a ∼day-long UV/blue signal, radio afterglow, and, if the jet escapes, an X-ray-flash (XRF)/low-luminosity gamma-ray-burst (llGRB) signal. For an O ( 1 ) PBH dark matter fraction and optimistic capture assumptions, the event rate can reach that of llGRBs. The low-mass, high-spin remnants offer a complementary PBH probe and possible source for subsolar black hole mergers.
population synthesis

Demographic inference in exoplanet chemistry is shaped by retrieval assumptions

Dwaipayan Dubey, Karan Molaverdikhani, Barbara Ercolano, Surya Shivaprasad et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过对20颗具有JWST传输光谱的系外行星进行大气化学反演,探究不同反演假设(自由化学与非平衡模型约束)对行星人口统计学化学推断的影响。结果显示,自由化学反演得到的C/O-平衡温度分布显现出非单调性,但这一表观规律缺乏统计稳健性且对样本组成敏感;而采用非平衡模型约束则得到更受限的C/O分布。研究表明行星群体尺度的化学趋势推断容易受反演参数化方式的强烈影响,应谨慎解读,并需明确检验样本选择、残差结构、先验敏感性等因素。
英文原摘要
Understanding the chemical diversity of exoplanet atmospheres is crucial for linking atmospheric composition to planetary formation history. Atmospheric C/O can provide clues about where and how planets form, particularly through the accretion of gas and solids across disk snowlines. Since planetary migration can change orbital distances and hence equilibrium temperatures ($T_{eq}$), investigating C/O across different planetary populations may provide insights into their formation and evolution. As observational precision improves with the JWST, the limits of standard atmospheric retrieval methods that assume altitude-independent molecular abundances have become apparent. We present atmospheric retrievals for 20 transiting planets with published JWST transmission spectra, including complementary HST spectra for targets where such data are available, spanning gas giants, warm Neptunes, and temperate/warm sub-Neptunes, to test how population-level chemical inferences depend on retrieval assumptions. Free-chemistry retrievals show a visually non-monotonic C/O-$T_{eq}$ distribution, but this apparent pattern is not statistically robust and is sensitive to sample composition. When molecular abundances are constrained by a grid of disequilibrium models, the inferred C/O distribution becomes more restricted, showing that demographic chemical trends can depend strongly on the adopted retrieval parameterization. Several hot Jupiters converge to low C/O ($\leq$ 0.05), whereas colder, cloudy planets retain broad posteriors that reflect limited information content of their spectra. Our results suggest that population-level chemical trends inferred from free-chemistry retrievals should be interpreted with caution unless sample selection, residual structure, prior sensitivity, and chemical assumptions are explicitly tested.

The Ionospheric Cut-off as a Potential Radio Technosignature

Michael A. Garrett · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过Chapman层模型调查行星电离层对宽带射电技术信号的频率截止效应。结果表明,不同宜居带行星的等离子体频率主要取决于恒星XUV辐照,典型截止频率为5-40 MHz,且该截止频率会随轨道周期产生40-50%的周期性变化,这种与轨道周期锁定的频率调制难以通过已知天体物理过程解释。该电离层截止特征可作为新型技术信号识别诊断工具,同时为系外行星电离层研究提供了新的探测手段。
英文原摘要
Radio emission generated on a planetary surface must cross the planet's ionosphere before escaping into space: below the local plasma frequency it is reflected back towards the surface. We investigate this process for broadband radio technosignatures (BRaTs) using a Chapman-layer description of photoionised atmospheres. The plasma frequency depends only weakly on stellar XUV irradiation, scaling as $Q_{\infty}^{1/4}$, implying turnover frequencies of 5-40 MHz for most terrestrial habitable-zone planets, potentially rising towards 100 MHz in extreme environments. The basic signature is simple: the continuum of a surface-origin BRaT should be strongly suppressed below a characteristic frequency that can be estimated from the XUV output of the host star, at every orbital phase and for any viewing geometry. Oblique propagation, magnetic fields, and collisional absorption broaden the cut-off into a smooth escape function, $f_{\mathrm{esc}}(ν,φ)$, where $φ$ is the orbital phase. Because the day-night ionisation pattern is anchored to the substellar point rather than to the rotating surface, disc integration adds a second, more discriminating signature: the half-escape frequency $ν_{1/2}$ varies predictably (by about 40-50 per cent over the orbit, depending on the angular emission pattern of the leakage) and remains locked to the orbital ephemeris. Finite atmospheric response times introduce modest phase lags but preserve the modulation. A low-frequency turnover alone is not unique to technology, but one that varies periodically with the orbital period would be difficult to reproduce through known astrophysical processes. Although observationally challenging, the ionospheric cut-off is a potential technosignature diagnostic and a novel probe of exoplanet ionospheres.

Transition Zone from ExoEarth to ExoVenus: Critical Insight from Planets on the Edge

Lisa Kaltenegger, Abigail Bohl, Connor Novak, Gillis Lowry · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过分析NASA系外行星档案库和盖亚DR3数据，识别并编制了23颗位于宜居带与金星带重叠区域（过渡带）的类地行星目标清单，以及2颗处于地-金星演化带的行星。过渡带行星可作为测试恒星辐照如何影响表面水保留和宜居性的天然实验室，从而揭示类地行星向类金星行星演化的机制。这项工作为JWST等新一代观测仪器的目标选择和观测策略提供了优化指导，有助于解答岩石行星宜居性出现与衰退的关键问题。
英文原摘要
Venus and Earth, planets with similar masses and radii, provide astonishingly different environments, raising the question of why these two rocky worlds evolved so differently. Explaining this divergence is a central challenge in comparative planetology and astrobiology. Now, atmospheric studies of exoplanets can explore the possibilities of terrestrial planet evolution, especially in the era of JWST; however, we have not yet realized the critical insights rocky exoplanets in the Transition Zone (TZ) can provide. These TZ planets in the overlap between the Habitable Zone and Venus Zone can test how incident stellar irradiation affects surface water retention and habitability. Observations of exoplanets in the TZ can probe not only the emergence and decline of exoplanet habitability but also test hypotheses about Venus' past, complementing upcoming Venus flagship missions. Here, we analyze NASA's Exoplanet Archive, updated with Gaia DR3 data, and provide a target list of 23 rocky exoplanets in the Earth-to-Venus Transition Zone, along with 2 planets in the Venus Evolution Zone (EV). We examine their demographics and quantify their potential for follow-up observations. This list of rocky TZ and EV planets allows observers to optimize target selection and observing strategy for specific instruments. Characterizing exoplanets in the Transition Zone will be critical to investigating open questions about the emergence and decline of habitability on rocky worlds, the evolution from an exo-Earth to an exo-Venus, and Earth's future.

The SPACE Program IV: Atmospheric Features on the Cold Transiting Sub-Neptune TOI-6478 b

Anastasia Triantafillides, Jason S. Pero, Angelique Kahle, Thomas Beatty et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究采用HST/WFC3观测低平衡温度(220 K)的亚海王星TOI-6478 b,通过考虑凌日光源效应排除恒星活动的假检测,首次在亚海王星上获得了~6σ的高信噪比光谱特征。自洽的大气模型表明1.4μm处的吸收特征主要来自CH₄而非H₂O,这与低温下H₂O凝聚冷阱的物理过程一致。研究表明HST/WFC3的窄波段难以区分主要吸收体,但JWST的宽波长覆盖将能够准确测量该目标的大气组成细节。
英文原摘要
Among the current population of exoplanets there are very few sub-Neptunes that have their atmospheric composition identified. This is primarily due to sub-Neptunes having high metallicity, clouds, and/or hazes obscuring absorption features. This often produces a spectrum that presents itself as a featureless spectrum especially in the near-IR. However, there are tentative trends that suggest sub-Neptunes with the lowest equilibrium temperatures ($\mathrm{T_{eq} < 400\:K}$) have detectable features, perhaps due to inefficient haze production or cloud sedimentation. TOI-6478 b is a low-density sub-Neptune exoplanet ($\rm <\mathrm{9.9\:M_{\oplus}}$ and $\rm \sim 4.5R_{\oplus}$) with an equilibrium temperature $\mathrm{T_{eq} = 220 \:K}$ assuming zero albedo. In this study, we present the highest S/N spectral feature ($\sim$6) of any sub-Neptune detected using HST/WFC3. We detect this spectral feature using a robust analysis that incorporates the Transit Light Source Effect (TLSE) to rule out false detections due to stellar activity. With the narrow bandpass of HST we were unable to distinguish whether the spectral feature at 1.4$μ$m is from H$_2$O or CH$_4$ absorption. Meanwhile, self-consistent atmospheric models provide physical motivation for CH$_4$ to be the dominant absorber given that H$_2$O should condense or cold trap at higher pressures. Through our analysis we determine that HST/WFC3 alone cannot distinguish between major absorbers. However, a broader wavelength coverage by JWST will be able to measure the atmospheric composition in detail, due to the high S/N features for this target.

Proxima Centauri’s Angular Diameter Revisited

Narsireddy Anugu, Alexandre Gallenne, Pierre Kervella, Adam D. Rains · The Astronomical Journal
本研究重新分析了比邻星五个VLBI/PIONIER干涉测量历元的数据，采用均匀圆盘和大气网格模型联合拟合，获得了更精确的角直径测量值。结果表明比邻星的半径为0.162±0.004 R⊙，有效温度为2833±49 K，其中近红外缘暗化修正引入2%-5%的系统差异。这些改进的恒星参数对于行星轨道建模和宜居性评估具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
Abstract Proxima Centauri is the nearest known exoplanet-hosting star and a benchmark for low-mass stellar models. Published diameters differ by several percent, affecting its inferred radius and temperature. Model-dependent near-infrared limb darkening introduces an additional 2%–5% correction to the inferred radius. We rereduced two previously published VLTI/PIONIER interferometric epochs from 2014 and combined them with three previously unpublished archival epochs from 2017 to 2019. We fit all five data sets jointly with uniform-disk and atmosphere-grid-based center-to-limb models. The joint fit gives a uniform-disk angular diameter, θ UD = 1.117 ± 0.024 mas. Using the PHOENIX model and adopted bolometric flux, we obtain a PHOENIX limb-darkened angular diameter, θ LD,PHX = 1.154 ± 0.026 mas, a stellar radius, R ⋆ = 0.162 ± 0.004 R ⊙ , and a Stefan–Boltzmann effective temperature, T eff,SB = 2833 ± 49 K. No significant close companion is detected across the five epochs over the searched separation and contrast range.

热木星与短周期行星1 篇

Potential spectral markers of intrinsic magnetic fields of hot exoplanets in the UV range

M. Rumenskikh, S. S. Sharipov, I. F. Shaikhislamov, A. E. Ivanova et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过原子取向效应（恒星各向异性辐射导致磁亚能级种群重新分布）适配了用Spektr-UV空间望远镜探测系外行星本征磁场的方法，并筛选出对取向效应敏感的紫外波段三重态吸收线。基于热木星KELT-20 b和HAT-P-32 b的三维气体动力学模型，计算了有无本征磁场条件下这些谱线的吸收光谱，通过Spektr-UV曝光时间计算器评估了观测可行性，提出了优化单次曝光时长和所需凌星次数的观测方案。
英文原摘要
A method for detecting the intrinsic magnetic fields of exoplanets, based on the effect of atomic alignment (the redistribution of populations among magnetic sublevels under the action of anisotropic radiation from the host star), has been adapted for observations with the Spektr-UV space telescope. A procedure for selecting sensitive transitions is proposed, and triplet absorption lines in the UV range that are sensitive to alignment are identified. On the basis of three-dimensional aeronomic models of the hot Jupiters KELT-20 b and HAT-P-32 b, absorption spectra in these lines are calculated for cases with and without intrinsic magnetic fields of the exoplanets. Using the Spektr-UV exposure-time calculator, the observability of the effect is assessed, and an observing program is proposed, specifying the optimal duration of individual exposures and the number of transits required to detect the signal.

Discovery of a Massive Outer Companion to the TOI-178 Resonant Chain

Emma V. Turtelboom, William Thompson, Ryan Cloutier, Alexandra Rochon et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
通过分析Gaia和径向速度数据的天体测量异常,我们在TOI-178系统中发现了一个位于数个AU处的大质量外伴星(TOI-178 h),其性质为质量21⁺⁴⁷₋₁₁木星质量、离心率0.76⁺⁰·¹⁸₋₀·₃₅的离心褐矮星。通过N体模拟和动力学稳定性分析,我们证明该外伴星的引力摄动下,内部五颗行星维持的2:4:6:9:12共振链仍保持稳定,表明该系统年龄已达数Gyr。TOI-178是已知唯一同时存在高重数共振链和大质量外伴星的系统,揭示了行星系统形成和演化的独特结果。
英文原摘要
We report the discovery of a massive outer companion around TOI-178, a known planetary system with six inner small planets within 0.15 AU, five of which are in a 2:4:6:9:12 resonant chain. We analyse the astrometric anomalies in the combined Gaia DR2/DR3 - Hipparcos catalogue (G23H) and literature radial velocities, revealing strong evidence for a massive outer companion at several AU. We combine the resulting orbital fit posterior with archival high-contrast imaging to constrain the properties of the corresponding massive outer companion, TOI-178 h. We also study the dynamical stability of the inner resonant chain in the presence of this previously unknown outer companion via both heuristic and N-body simulations. We find that the inner system appears to be robust to the perturbing influence of TOI-178\,h, in line with the systems several Gyr age. Our analysis results in a final posterior solution for TOI-178\,h with a semi-major axis of $6.9^{+12.3}_{-3.3}$ AU, a mass of $21^{+47}_{-11} M_J$, and eccentricity of $0.76^{+0.18}_{-0.35}$, likely corresponding to an eccentric brown dwarf. TOI-178 is currently the only known system that has maintained a high-multiplicity resonant chain whilst in the dynamical presence of a massive outer companion, showcasing a unique outcome of planet formation.

Thermodynamics of gas giants accreting via shocks: the role of molecular hydrogen dissociation

Bingjie Zha, Zhuo Chen · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究在核心吸积框架下，通过耦合辐射流体力学和H₂解离物理，使用物理信息神经网络(PINN)预测气体巨星吸积激波的热力学条件，并将其作为行星内部演化模型的边界条件。研究表明，相比传统冷启动模型，考虑激波辐射冷却和H₂解离效应后，气体巨星的激波后温度、熵跳跃和初始光度显著更高（提高4-10倍），且行星磁场强度随质量增加而显著增强（从0.3 MJ的几高斯到3 MJ的~500高斯）。这一改进的激波热力学模型为直接成像系外行星的质量和吸积率约束提供了更准确的预测。
英文原摘要
Within the core-accretion framework, gas giants acquire most of their mass through accretion shocks during runaway gas accretion. The shock thermodynamics---governed by radiative cooling, accretion history, and equation of state (EoS)---sets the post-shock gas entropy and initial thermal state, strongly influencing both observables and subsequent evolution. We aim to build a coupled evolutionary model predicting the initial thermodynamic conditions, intrinsic luminosities, and upper limits on magnetic field strengths of gas giants across diverse accretion histories. We couple radiation hydrodynamics and $\mathrm{H_2}$ dissociation to long-term planetary interior evolution by training a physics-informed neural network (PINN) predictor on 1000 first-principles simulations resolving the shock physics. This PINN provides post-shock outer boundary conditions for MESA to evolve the planetary envelope beyond runaway accretion. The PINN predicts higher post-shock temperatures than the canonical cold-start model, with the discrepancy widening with planetary mass, while post-shock entropy jumps sharply when $\mathrm{H_2}$ dissociates at $\sim 2000$ K. Gas giants evolved with PINN-derived boundary conditions achieve intrinsic luminosities 4--10 times higher than cold-start models. Under optimistic assumptions, the maximum magnetic field strength at $2 R_{\mathrm{J}}$ reaches a few gauss for a $0.3 M_{\mathrm{J}}$ planet, $\sim 100$ G for $1 M_{\mathrm{J}}$, and $\sim 500$ G for $3 M_{\mathrm{J}}$. Incorporating detailed radiation hydrodynamics and realistic thermodynamics into accretion shock models yields higher initial entropies, brighter intrinsic luminosities, and stronger magnetic fields, providing improved constraints on the masses and accretion rates of directly imaged exoplanets.

A Comparative Study of the Streaming Instability: Unstratified Models with Marginally Coupled Grains

Stanley A. Baronett, Wladimir Lyra, Hossam Aly, Olivia Brouillette et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究采用七种流体力学数值代码(包括有限体积、有限差分以及拉格朗日粒子和无压流体尘埃模型)进行了流式不稳定性的首次系统对比,在无分层模型和单位停止时间条件下研究了该不稳定性的物理特性。所有代码都再现了指数增长、丝状结构形成和湍流饱和的特征演化过程,但在中等分辨率下,尘埃模型是造成数值差异的主要来源,粒子模型达到更高的峰值密度;随着分辨率提高至1024²,不同模型间的差异显著减小。研究表明,只有统计诊断量才能在不同数值代码之间进行有意义的比较,为行星小体形成提供了更可靠的物理约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract The streaming instability is a leading mechanism for concentrating solids and initiating planetesimal formation in protoplanetary disks. Although numerous studies have explored its linear growth, nonlinear evolution, and implications for planet formation, the diversity of numerical methods and dust treatments used across the literature has made it difficult to assess which features of the instability are physically robust and which arise from code-dependent choices. We present the first systematic comparison of seven hydrodynamic codes—spanning finite-volume and finite-difference schemes and modeling dust either as Lagrangian particles or as a pressureless fluid—applied to the unstratified streaming instability with a dimensionless stopping time of unity. All codes reproduce the characteristic sequence of exponential growth, filament formation, and turbulent saturation, demonstrating broad agreement in the qualitative behavior of the instability. Quantitatively, however, the dust model remains the dominant source of variation at moderate resolution; particle-based simulations reach higher peak densities and exhibit broader high-density tails than fluid-based models at 512 2 resolution, although increasing the number of particles brings their initial maximum density evolution into close agreement with that of dust-fluid models. At 1024 2 , these differences diminish substantially, indicating better agreement of the saturated-state statistics across dust treatments. In terms of computational performance, most particle implementations suffer from imbalanced parallelized loads, while execution on a GPU is at least 2–3 times more energy efficient and scales better at higher resolutions than on CPUs. Given the intrinsic stochasticity of this nonlinear system, only statistical diagnostics remain meaningful across codes.

Collisional Evolution around A-type Stars with Strong Silicate Features

T. A. Stuber, H. Kobayashi, S. Wolf, V. Faramaz-Gorka · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过碰撞动力学模拟（而非传统幂律分布模型）研究了A型星周围陆地区域的小行星带演化，考虑辐射压对尘埃粒度分布的影响。模拟结果表明，辐射压修正的碰撞级联可以再现观测到的硅酸盐光谱特征，且较小粒子虽有贡献但不主导光谱，通过系统年龄分析推断年轻系统的小行星带可源自原行星盘，而年老系统则可能经历过近期巨型碰撞事件或尘埃通过Poynting-Robertson阻力内漂。
英文原摘要
Abstract Around luminous A-type stars, retaining micrometer-sized dust grains is difficult owing to the high radiation pressure that repels particles with sizes below the blowout limit. Yet such particles have been evidenced in the terrestrial zone around those stars, via pronounced silicate spectral features. An in situ planetesimal belt that collisionally replenishes the dust is expected to deplete quickly, limiting the lifetime of observable features. However, modeling of such systems often relies on a power-law description of the grain size distribution that has a lower size limit owing to blowout. Using more complex, collisional simulations instead, we investigated the temporal evolution of hypothetical planetesimal belts in the terrestrial zone around five A-type stars showing strong spectral silicate features. A collisional cascade that is modified by radiation pressure results in dust distributions that replicate observed spectral features without requiring other physical processes. While features can be altered by particles below the blowout limit, their magnitude is dominated by the emission of larger grains. Using the systems’ ages, we investigated the origin of the planetesimal belt. For the three younger systems of our sample, a planetesimal belt that emerged from the protoplanetary disk phase can explain the observations. This is even possible for two older systems if the planetesimals are located at slightly larger distances than the dust, e.g., if the dust drifted inward owing to the Poynting–Robertson drag. Alternatively, a recent production of the planetesimals, for instance, in a giant collision event, could have happened only several million years ago.

exoALMA XXV: A Statistical Analysis of Gaseous Substructure in Low-inclination Disks

Richard Teague, Sean M. Andrews, Jaehan Bae, Marcelo Barraza-Alfaro et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究对exoALMA项目的四个低倾角原行星盘应用二阶结构函数统计分析其气体速度和温度扰动,排除了大于~1.5压力标度高的非轴对称结构,发现盘内存在沿方位角方向被开普勒旋转剪切的小尺度相关结构。研究引入模态功率谱和卷绕对比两个新统计量来表征螺旋结构,其中MWC 758的温度场显示出与翘曲照明一致的m=1尾随结构,速度场则携带可能重新分配角动量的小尺度剪切结构。这些无需追踪臂峰的统计量将对旋臂卷绕的定量描述扩展到絮状体系,为研究盘内复杂的气体动力学结构提供了新方法。
英文原摘要
Abstract Protoplanetary disks exhibit complex structure in both their gas dynamics and their temperature. Characterizing it has proven challenging, owing to the mismatch between the observed complexity and the relatively simple semianalytical models typically used to interpret it. To augment these analyses, we apply the second-order structure function to the velocity and temperature perturbations of four low-inclination sources from the exoALMA program, each measured against a noise-only null built from the data themselves. All exhibit correlated structure. The radial correlation length is limited by the beam in every case, which excludes nonaxisymmetric structure on radial scales larger than ∼1.5 pressure scale heights. In three of the four disks, the structure is instead resolved and elongated in azimuth by factors of 1.6–2.3, as expected of small-scale perturbations sheared out by Keplerian rotation. We further introduce two statistics that characterize spiral structure without tracing an arm crest: a modal power spectrum, measuring the periodicity of the azimuthal structure, and a winding contrast , measuring the radial dependence of its phase. The velocity residuals of three disks are significantly wound. MWC 758 is the only source in which the winding is coherent across the disk and shared by the temperature field. Its trailing m = 1 temperature structure is consistent with the illumination of a warp, while its velocity field carries small-scale, sheared structure that could redistribute angular momentum. Because neither statistic requires a crest, both extend a quantitative description of winding to the flocculent regime, where a spiral cannot be parameterized.

NEXONN: Advancing the NEXOTRANS Exoplanet Atmospheric Retrieval Framework with Transformer-Based Neural Posterior Estimation for JWST Transmission Spectroscopy of WASP-39b

Tasneem Basra Khan, Liton Majumdar, Mithun Patra, Tonmoy Deka et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究开发了NEXONN框架——一个基于Transformer的神经后验估计方法，用于快速进行JWST凌星光谱的系外行星大气成分反演，并完整量化不确定性。通过在WASP-39b的多波段JWST观测数据（0.6-12微米）上进行基准测试，NEXONN与传统贝叶斯方法（PyMultiNest）得到一致的大气参数结果（如C/O比和金属丰度），且推断速度提升数个数量级。该方法使得从单个行星到行星种群的大气统计研究成为可能，为系外行星形成演化的群体统计学研究提供了高效工具。
英文原摘要
The James Webb Space Telescope is delivering unprecedented constraints on exoplanet atmospheres, but computationally intensive Bayesian retrievals challenge the transition from individual planets to population-level studies of planetary formation and evolution. We introduce NEXONN, a transformer-conditioned normalizing-flow framework within NEXOTRANS that enables rapid atmospheric posterior estimation with uncertainty quantification. Incorporating PandExo-based JWST noise, we benchmark NEXONN against PyMultiNest using simulated spectra and JWST observations of WASP-39 b (0.6-12 microns). For simulated MIRI/LRS data, eight posteriors achieve a mean overlap of 80.32%. Retrievals of combined NIRISS, NIRSpec, and MIRI observations yield consistent atmospheric properties. For the full dataset, NEXONN infers C/O = 0.82 (+0.01/-0.02) and [M/H] = 1.13 (+0.04/-0.04), compared with PyMultiNest values of 0.80 (+0.03/-0.01) and 1.19 (+0.05/-0.03). Divergence metrics, Wasserstein distances, posterior overlaps, and coverage tests assess posterior fidelity and uncertainty calibration, revealing parameter-dependent differences. Once trained, NEXONN performs posterior inference within seconds, enabling scalable population studies of exoplanet atmospheric composition, formation, and evolution.

MAIC-2 v2.3: A Fortran program for simulating the surface temperature, atmospheric water transport and surface glaciation of the planet Mars

Ralf Greve, Björn Grieger, Oliver J. Stenzel · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
MAIC-2 v2.3是一个高效的纬向平均模型,用于模拟火星表面温度、大气水输运和冰川作用在10^5-10^7年尺度上的演变过程。该模型采用物理基础的参数化方案(包括局部辐照温度方案和由自由对流驱动的物质平衡引擎),计算成本远低于全球环流模式,适于研究百万年尺度的轨道演化影响。通过2000万年的瞬态模拟验证,MAIC-2能够成功再现火星极地分层沉积物的主要特征,为行星气候长期演化研究提供了开源工具。
英文原摘要
The Mars Atmosphere-Ice Coupler MAIC-2 is a computationally efficient, zonally-averaged model designed to simulate Martian surface temperature, atmospheric water transport and surface glaciation over timescales of 10^5 to 10^7 years. While sophisticated General Circulation Models (GCMs) provide high-resolution simulations of the Martian atmosphere, their high computational cost makes them largely unsuitable for studies covering millions of years of orbital evolution. MAIC-2 addresses this gap by utilizing physically-based, zonally-averaged parameterizations, including a Local Insolation Temperature (LIT) scheme and a mass balance engine driven by free-convection and instantaneous mixing physics. Version 2.3 introduces a fully modularized Fortran architecture that improves maintainability and ensures that the software remains entirely self-contained, requiring no external numerical libraries or specialized data formats for execution. Demonstrated through a 20-million-year transient simulation, the model successfully reproduces the primary characteristics of the Martian polar layered deposits. MAIC-2 provides the planetary science community with a versatile, open-source tool for investigating the long-term climatic impacts of orbital variations on the Martian surface.

What’s in Your Transit? A Path to More Science from Exoplanet Transit Data

Samson J. Mercier, Julien de Wit, Benjamin V. Rackham · The Astronomical Journal
本研究量化了凌日深度测量中由于凌边昏暗效应和轨道参数同时反演而产生的精度损失问题,通过对500,000+个恒星强度剖面的注入-回收测试表明,凌边昏暗系数是引入超额不确定性的主要因素。研究发现三阶多项式和四阶非线性凌边昏暗律相比广泛使用的二阶律能提供更好的精度-准确性权衡,而改进的恒星模型可使凌边昏暗系数先验约束更严格,从而将凌日深度不确定性降低1.1-2.5倍,相当于减少1-6倍的凌日观测次数。
英文原摘要
Abstract Transit-depth measurements can remain less precise than expected from photon noise alone, because limb darkening and orbital parameters are inferred simultaneously with transit depth. We refer to this shortfall as the “transit-depth precision problem” and quantify it with an amplification factor, A , comparing the measured transit-depth uncertainty with baseline scatter. Using central-transit injection–retrieval tests informed by >500,000 MPS-ATLAS intensity profiles, we determine how limb-darkening prescriptions and priors affect both transit-depth precision and accuracy. We find that limb-darkening coefficients are the dominant contributors to the excess uncertainty, followed by inclination and stellar density, through parameter correlations. In the configurations tested, the widely used quadratic law can produce low A yet substantially biased transit depths and wavelength-dependent structure in otherwise flat transmission spectra. Third-order polynomial and fourth-order nonlinear laws provide a better precision–accuracy tradeoff, although all quantitative results remain conditional on the adopted stellar models and transit geometry. Improved stellar models can increase observing efficiency by enabling tighter, accurate limb-darkening coefficient priors: in our fourth-order nonlinear tests, 10%–20% priors reduce transit-depth uncertainties by factors of 1.1–1.8 relative to uniform priors, while stronger 1% priors reduce them by 2.1–2.5. Assuming uncertainty decreases as the inverse square root of the number of observed transits, these gains correspond to reaching the same target transit-depth precision with approximately 1–3 and 4–6 times fewer transits, respectively. We recommend testing multiple limb-darkening prescriptions and reporting wavelength-resolved sensitivity to limb-darkening assumptions.

Mapping of interstellar ices with JWST in protostellar envelopes:. Spatial variations of 13 CO_2$, ^12CO_2, CO, OCN^-, and H$_2O ice

N. G. C. Brunken, E. F. van Dishoeck, A. C. A. Boogert, P. Nazari et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究使用JWST NIRSpec IFU对低质量原星L1527和高质量原星IRAS 20126+4104的原星包层进行空间分辨光谱观测，通过绘制13CO2、12CO2、CO、OCN-和H2O冰的光学厚度和丰度分布图，揭示了冰成分的非均匀分布与温度结构的耦合关系。研究发现两个原星的包层内均存在局部温度区和子结构，CO2冰发生分层、OCN-相对于CO2富集，且这些特征与喷流冲击和吸积盘区域的温度升高密切相关，表明行星形成环境中冰的物理化学演化受控于包层的温度结构。
英文原摘要
The composition of planets and their atmospheres is largely determined by the environment in which the planet accretes material. This environment is pre-determined during early stages when the protostar is still forming and impacting the complexity of the material in the envelope. Charting the structure of protostellar envelopes is therefore crucial for understanding how the chemistry is linked to the physical processes occurring in stellar nurseries, and JWST offers a unique opportunity to perform spatial mapping at a sub-arcsec resolution. We aim to explore spatial variations of the main ice constituents in the envelope of the low-mass protostar L1527 and the high-mass protostar IRAS 20126+4104 using JWST observations. We used JWST NIRSpec IFU observations to extract spectra at each pixel in the IFU of 13 CO_2$,^12CO_2, CO, OCN^-, and H_2O ice features. We created optical depth and spatial abundance maps to study the distribution of the ices and use the ^13CO$_2 ice feature, a known thermal tracer, to identify regions of high temperatures where the ices are thermally processed. Our findings show that the distribution of ices in both L1527 and IRAS 20126+4104 is not uniform, with both envelopes showing localized temperature zones and substructures. In L1527, CO_2 ice segregation and OCN-$ enhancement with respect to CO_2 are observed in the warm central disk lane close to the protostar. In IRAS 20126+4104, the warm temperature zones are observed mostly towards the shocked region in the NW outflow where dense material is interacting with the precessing jet as well as close to the central source. The CO_2 ice is segregated at these locations and we observe sublimation of CO ice, as well as strong gaseous CO rotation vibrational lines, especially in the shocked NW outflow. Close to the central protostar we see an additional enhancement of OCN^- with respect to CO_2 that also points towards elevated temperatures and crystalline H$_2O ice is also detected at a few locations that coincide with shock knots in the outflow. In both the low-mass protostar and the high-mass one, the findings indicate that the distribution of ices and their physical structure are linked to the temperature structure of the envelopes. The envelope of IRAS 20126 shows a more complex structure, where interacting material results in localized regions of high temperatures and denser regions that harbor cooler ices. L1527, in contrast, appears to be a more structured system, with heated warm ices observed mostly towards the central disk regions.

Impacts of planetary rotation and winds on the distribution and escape of the Martian hydrogen corona

Wenlong Li, Hao Gu, Jun Cui, Jiang Yu et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过三维Chamberlain模型和修正的Jeans逃逸公式，定量评估了行星自转和大气风场对火星氢逃逸及日球晕分布的个体和耦合效应。结果表明，虽然水平风场和自转对全球整合逃逸通量的影响较小（H变化数个百分点，H₂约10%），但局地效应显著（H增强~24%，H₂超100%），且这种效应在H₂和太阳最小值期间更为显著，并导致1000-4000 km高度范围内的日球晕密度分布产生可观的三维结构变化。这项研究强调动力学效应在解释火星氢逃逸时空变化和日球晕观测中的重要性。
英文原摘要
The escape of hydrogen produced from the photodissociation of atmospheric water-bearing species is a major pathway through which Mars has lost water over geological timescales. Although thermal escape is primarily controlled by the density and temperature of hydrogen-bearing species near the exobase, horizontal atmospheric motions can modify the particle velocity distribution and thereby affect both escape and the structure of the extended hydrogen corona. Previous studies have considered the effects of thermospheric winds, but the contribution of planetary rotation and its interaction with atmospheric winds remain poorly quantified. We aim to investigate the individual and combined effects of horizontal atmospheric winds and planetary rotation on the thermal escape and exospheric distributions of H and H_2 at Mars. We incorporated spatially varying exobase conditions, horizontal winds, and planetary rotation into a three-dimensional Chamberlain exosphere model and a bulk-motion-modified Jeans escape formulation. Calculations were performed under solar-minimum and solar-maximum conditions at three representative orbital positions near aphelion, equinox, and perihelion. We compared classical Jeans, wind-only, rotation-only, and combined wind-plus-rotation cases to quantify changes in local and globally integrated escape fluxes and in the three-dimensional exospheric density structure. Horizontal winds and planetary rotation produce only modest changes in the globally integrated escape rates, with H varying by only a few percent and H_2 by approximately 10%. Their combined effects are not simply additive because the wind and rotational velocities combine vectorially and may reinforce or partially cancel each other. Locally, however, the enhancements are much larger, reaching approximately 24% for H and more than 100% for H_2. The response is stronger for H_2 and under solar minimum conditions because the horizontal bulk velocity represents a larger fraction of the particle thermal speed. Horizontal bulk motion also modifies the exospheric density distribution, with relative differences generally increasing with altitude. For the rotation-only case at (L_s=70^̧irc), the maximum density enhancement at 4000 km reaches about 6--13% for H and 26--60% for H_2, depending on solar activity. Atmospheric winds and planetary rotation play a secondary role in controlling the globally integrated thermal escape of H and H_2, but they can produce substantial regional variations in escape flux and localized changes in the modeled exospheric densities. These effects are more pronounced for H_2 and are produced under colder atmospheric conditions, with the relative density differences generally increasing with altitude over the investigated range of 1000--4000 km above the Martian surface. These dynamical effects should therefore be considered when interpreting spatial and temporal variations in hydrogen escape and observations of the extended Martian corona.

A Sample of Thick-disk Stars Hosting Hot Jupiters

Victoria DiTomasso, Samuel W. Yee, Romy Rodríguez Martínez, Emily K. Pass et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究对268颗确认和候选热木星主星进行了同源光谱和测光分析，利用化学丰度比（[α/Fe]和[Fe/H]）将其分类为银河系薄盘、厚盘或中间族群，并鉴别出5颗新发现的厚盘热木星系统。研究发现厚盘热木星的质量和轨道周期与薄盘样本存在显著差异，表明热木星的形成可能受到银河环境和恒星年龄的影响。这个超过8 Gyr的古老系统样本为理解热木silon在不同星系环境中的形成机制和长期轨道演化提供了独特的观测窗口。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present a homogeneous analysis of 268 confirmed and candidate transiting hot-Jupiter host stars with 4800 K < T eff < 6200 K. We combine high-resolution TRES spectra, Gaia, Two Micron All Sky Survey (2MASS) and Wide-field Infrared Survey Explorer (WISE) photometry, and Gaia astrometry to determine these stars’ T eff , log g , R ⋆ , [ α /Fe], and [Fe/H]. Based on their [ α /Fe] and [Fe/H], we classify the stars as members of the Milky Way’s low-[ α /Fe] thin disk, high-[ α /Fe] thick disk or an ambiguous intermediate population. We identify two confirmed hot Jupiters (WASP-81 b and TOI-5322 b) and three candidate hot Jupiters (TOI-3118.01, TOI-2728.01, and TOI-5781.01) as orbiting previously unrecognized members of the high-[ α/Fe] thick disk. We compare the properties of the confirmed planets in the three populations and note two indications of a potential difference between the populations: both high-[ α /Fe] confirmed planets and eight out of the 11 ambiguous-disk confirmed planets are less massive than the median low-[ α /Fe] planet, and none of our high-[ α /Fe] or ambiguous confirmed planets have orbital periods shorter than 2.7 day, when 22% of our low-[ α /Fe] population do. These differences may reflect variations in giant-planet formation across galactic environments, evolutionary effects, or differing stellar properties between the high- and low-[ α /Fe] populations. Our catalog is the largest sample of chemically identified high-[ α /Fe] thick-disk confirmed and candidate hot-Jupiter host stars. These ancient systems, with ages > 8 Gyr, may allow for a better understanding of how the formation of hot Jupiters depends on galactic environment, and how their orbits evolve over very long time scales.

Planets Surviving Common-envelope Evolution Should Be Rare, Massive, and Orbit Very Close to Their Host Star

Yadira S. Gaibor, Andrew Vanderburg, Juliette C. Becker, Melinda Soares-Furtado et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过模拟巨行星系统在主序星演化至白矮星过程中的共同包层演化，预测经此机制存活行星的发生率和轨道-质量分布。结果表明共同包层存活者应为稀有、高质量(>10木星质量)且轨道极近(≲0.006天文单位)的行星，这与已知白矮星周围行星WD 1856+534 b的性质不符，暗示该行星可能经历了不同的形成机制。
英文原摘要
Abstract The discovery of exoplanets around white dwarfs uniquely informs our understanding of planetary survival through stellar evolution. Common-envelope (CE) evolution is one of the proposed mechanisms that could shape the orbital architecture of these planets around stellar remnants. In this work, we predict the occurrence rate and orbital-separation/planet-mass distributions for planets that could form around white dwarfs through CE evolution. We simulate populations of giant-planet systems, sampling initial conditions from observed mass and semimajor-axis distributions for main-sequence stars. We evolve the host star from main sequence to white dwarf to assess which planetary configurations could survive, and we predict the resulting post-CE planet population. Our results suggest that such planets should be rare, massive, and have very close orbital separations. We find that most of the surviving planets have masses of >10 M Jup and orbital separations <1.4 times the tidal-disruption radius, a disrupt (≲0.006 au, also called the Roche limit), which is in contrast to high-eccentricity migration, which produces planets with orbital separations >2 a disrupt . The properties of CE survivors from our simulations contrast with the known closely orbiting planet WD 1856+534 b, suggesting a different mechanism may be responsible for its formation.

Unveiling the mutual inclination distribution in astrometric surveys

Jean-Baptiste Delisle · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对Gaia第四次数据发布中三体系统轨道解的相互倾角简并性问题，提出了分层贝叶斯框架来统计推断种群级的相互倾角分布。由于天体测量仅对天球面内的运动敏感，每个三体系统的轨道解存在两个可能的相互倾角值，该框架通过联合处理多系统数据来消除这一简并性。在模拟数据集上的验证表明该方法能可靠地恢复注入的相互倾角分布，为Gaia DR4三体系统的统计分析提供了有力工具。
英文原摘要
Mutual inclination in three (or more) body systems (triple stars, circumbinary planets, multi-planetary systems, etc.) is a tracer of their formation and evolution. Gaia Data Release 4 will provide for the first time a catalog of orbital solutions for three-body systems. Because astrometry is only sensitive to the motion in the plane of the sky, there is a degeneracy in the orbital solutions leading to two possible solutions for the mutual inclination in three-body systems. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian framework to circumvent this degeneracy and unveil the mutual inclination distribution in the population. We tested our methods on simulated datasets, and showed that it reliably recovered the injected mutual inclination distributions in various cases. The method is thus promising for the statistical analysis of Gaia DR4 three-body systems.
astrometry · astrometric

Brown dwarfs and stars are dynamically indistinguishable as companions in AU-separation binaries

Ben Pennell, Johanna Müller-Horn, Sahar Shahaf, Hans-Walter Rix et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究从Gaia DR3中组建了368个主序星-褐矮星AU级分离联星的样本,通过XP分光测光和FEROS光谱消除了近等质量双星的污染。研究发现褐矮星伴星的轨道离心率分布、金属丰度分布及质量分布均与恒星伴星相似,表明AU级分离的褐矮星伴星主要通过类似恒星双星的形成通道产生,而非类行星通道。这一结果表明褐矮星与恒星在动力学上可区分性很低,且褐矮星形成的质量下限至少延伸至20木星质量。
英文原摘要
Brown dwarfs occupy the mass range between stars and planets, populations whose dominant formation channels differ, which leave measurable differences in the orbital eccentricity and host-star metallicity distributions. We assemble a sample of astrometric binaries with main sequence primaries and brown dwarf companions at $\mathcal{O}$(AU) separations from $\textit{Gaia}$ DR3. The observable photocenter motions for such binaries are degenerate between a genuinely low-mass companion and a near-equal-mass binary. We break this degeneracy using $\textit{Gaia}$ XP spectrophotometry and follow-up FEROS spectroscopy, which removes removes ${\sim}70\%$ of initial low-mass-function candidates as likely near-twin binaries and leaves residual contamination of only a few percent. The resulting 368 systems, with masses and metallicities determined from the XP spectra and a selection function forward-modelled with $\texttt{Gaiamock}$, form the largest homogeneous sample of brown dwarf companions with astrometric orbits. We find the eccentricity distribution to be well described by a normal distribution. This is comparable to eccentricities of stellar companions at the same periods and higher than those of giant planets, with no significant dependence on companion mass ($α=-0.13\pm0.09$) or period ($β=0.10\pm0.05$). The host-star metallicity distribution is likewise stellar-like, extending to [M/H]$=-0.5$, in contrast to the predominantly super-solar metallicity hosts of giant planets. After accounting for selection, the companion-mass distribution over all brown dwarf masses is consistent with an extension of stellar-binary demographics. Brown dwarf companions at AU separations down to at least $20\,M_J$ therefore appear to form predominantly through a binary-star-like channel. A possible transition to planet-like formation at lower masses cannot be located with the present sample.

NEMESIS: Exoplanet TraNsit SurvEy of Nearby M-dwarfs in TESS FFIS II - 262 Transiting Planet Candidates from the TESS Primary Mission

Dax L. Feliz, Madeline Maldonado Gutierrez, Maliyah Adams, Shreeya KC et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究采用NEMESIS盲搜管道对TESS主任务扇形图像中的113,047颗100 pc内的M矮星进行系统性凌日搜索,通过自动化LEO-Vetter筛选和TRICERATOPS贝叶斯分析,从23,339个阈值触发事件中确认了262个行星候选体(其中232个为新发现),周期在0.51-9.10天范围内。经过多重检验和假警报估计,推断1.0-10.0天周期和0.5-11.0地球半径范围内的行星丰度为每星0.81±0.44颗,显著扩展了M矮星周围短周期小行星的统计样本。
英文原摘要
We present NEMESIS II, a blind transit search of nearby M-dwarfs in TESS Primary Mission full-frame images (Sectors 1-26), including stars lacking 2-minute observations. Using the open-source NEMESIS pipeline, we extracted, detrended, and searched 213,658 single-sector light curves of 113,047 M-dwarfs within 100 pc, yielding 23,339 threshold crossing events. Automated LEO-Vetter vetting, applied identically to the survey and its injection-recovery and orbit-scrambling controls, retains 558 detections corresponding to 525 distinct signals with converged transit fits and periods of 0.51-9.10 days. Of these, 482 lie on stars without TOI, CTOI, or NASA Exoplanet Archive transiting-planet entries. Orbit-scrambling predicts 329 false alarms among the 558 detections, all but 14 of them among the 408 below Multiple Event Statistic 8. Assuming an astrophysical signal, TRICERATOPS favors a planetary origin for 262 of 525 candidates (232 new), which we classify as planet candidates; it also excludes resolved-neighbor eclipses for 105. Known planets and TOI candidates survive vetting at 1.01 +/- 0.07 of the injection-predicted rate. Correcting for each light curve's sensitivity, false alarms, and astrophysical false positives, we infer 0.81 +/- 0.44 planets per star for periods between 1.0-10.0 days and planet radii between 0.5-11.0 Earth radii. This is a lower limit, because our injections recover few or no planets near and below 1 Earth radii and assume central transits. Light curves, catalogs, vetting reports, and injection-recovery results will be released as a High Level Science Product at MAST upon publication. Documentation for the NEMESIS pipeline is available at https://nemesis-tess.readthedocs.io/.
transit survey · planet candidate

The JWST Sub-Jupiters Survey: Observed Sensitivities to the Wide-Separation, Cold, Sub-Jupiter Exoplanet Population

Giovanni M. Strampelli, Aarynn L. Carter, Beth Biller, Clémence Fontanive et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过灵敏度分析三个JWST直接成像项目,评估了其对邻近年轻星协中0.1-1木星质量亚木星系外行星的探测能力,发现这些项目可探测有效温度低至~250 K、分离度~20-500 au范围内的系外行星,其质量灵敏度优于现有地面观测。研究通过将测光衍生性质与理论模型预测相关联,揭示了探测亚土星到木星质量系外行星的可行性强烈依赖于行星大气化学成分的预期特征,为未来系外行星探测提供了解释框架。
英文原摘要
We present a sensitivity analysis of three recent JWST direct imaging programs (GO-4050, GO-5835, and SURVEY-6005) designed to build a demographic study of sub-Jupiter mass ($0.1 - 1$ M$_{\mathrm{J}}$) exoplanets in nearby young associations and moving groups. We find that these programs are sensitive to sub-Saturn to Jupiter mass exoplanets with effective temperature (T$_\mathrm{eff}$) as low as $\lesssim 250$ K, already at $\sim 2$ arcsec. A comparison with the SHINE survey confirms that the improvements in mass sensitivity provided by JWST high-contrast imaging surveys outpace current ground observation for masses $\lesssim 1$ M$_{\mathrm{J}}$ and separations between $\sim20-500$ au, opening up a novel parameter space to explore. Overall, this analysis provides a robust framework for interpreting future exoplanet detections. By correlating photometrically derived properties (e.g. mass, $T_{\mathrm{eff}}$, and separation) with theoretical model predictions, we show how the access to potentially low detectable masses (sub-Saturn to Jupiter) strongly depends on the expected atmospheric chemistry of the exoplanet population.

A First Look at Astrometric Outer Companions to Super-Earths and Sub-Neptunes using Gaia DR2 and DR3

Alexandra Rochon, Emma V. Turtelboom, Ryan Cloutier, William Thompson et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用Gaia DR2-DR3的精确天体测量数据,对7321颗FGKM型矮星进行系统搜索,以量化超地球和亚海王星系统中外伴星的出现率相对于背景的超额。通过Octofitter轨道拟合框架和贝叶斯分层模型,研究发现这些内小行星系统中存在显著的外伴星超额($1.77\pm0.49$),与径向速度巡天结果独立一致,且超地球和亚海王星系统中的超额相似,表明外伴星不会抑制内小行星形成。这些结果支持原位外形成模型,表明内小行星在外伴星打开间隙之前在更大分离处快速形成并随后通过I型迁移演化,同时发现M矮星周围的外伴星超额强于FGK矮星。
英文原摘要
Massive outer companions (OCs) are believed to promote, inhibit, or otherwise regulate the formation and composition of inner small planets (ISPs) both in the solar system and in exoplanetary systems. To assess how, we leverage calibrated absolute astrometry from Gaia DR2-DR3 of 7,321 FGKM dwarfs and present measurements of the relative occurrence rate of OCs in systems hosting known ISPs (i.e. P(OC$|$ISP)/P(OC)), including systems with inner super-Earths only, inner sub-Neptunes only, and across a range of spectral types and stellar metallicities. We search for OCs in pairs of planet and reference stellar samples using the orbital fitting framework Octofitter and characterize the occurrence of OCs in mass--semi-major axis space under a Bayesian hierarchical model. We find an excess of OCs in systems with ISPs ($1.77\pm0.49$), independently supporting results from radial velocity surveys for the first time using astrometry. We recover similar excesses in both super-Earth (SE; $2.02\pm0.52$) and sub-Neptune systems (SN; $1.97\pm0.50$), highlighting that OCs do not inhibit the formation of ISPs, regardless of planet type. We argue that the consistency across SE and SN systems supports ex-situ formation, wherein ISPs form rapidly at larger separations prior to OCs opening disk gaps, thus providing evidence of Type I migration of SEs and SNs. We also evaluate how these results vary across stellar mass and metallicity, finding that M dwarfs exhibit an OC excess that is $1.41\pm0.78\times$ stronger than that around FGK dwarfs, and not finding a significant dependence on metallicity, contrary to previous radial velocity surveys.

Validation of Ariel candidates orbiting K-M stars

Giulia Piccinini, Claudia Di Maio, Giuseppina Micela, Jonathan Brande et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究采用TRICERATOPS工具对318个环绕K-M型星的Ariel任务候选行星进行统计验证,结合Gaia DR3的双星指标(RUWE和IPD_frac_multi_peak)进行系统的假阳性排除分析。研究验证了56个行星候选体、排除了29个假阳性并确认35个可能的行星,为Ariel任务提供了概率优先级排序以优化后续观测和大气特征研究的目标选择。这项工作通过扩展已确认行星的参数空间覆盖范围,为行星群体统计研究提供了重要的样本补充。
英文原摘要
One of the key goals of planetary population studies is to achieve broad coverage of the parameter space, which is not yet fully represented by confirmed planets. This limitation can be mitigated by including planets that have been statistically validated through a vetting procedure, even if they are not fully confirmed. In preparation for the Ariel mission, a Mission Candidate Sample has been assembled, comprising both confirmed planets and several unvalidated candidates. In this context, we apply a vetting analysis to a selected subsample of these candidates. Our methodology is based on the TRICERATOPS tool, aimed at confirming the presence of transiting planets or ruling out false-positive scenarios. We applied TRICERATOPS to a sample of 318 targets drawn from the last three Ariel TESS Planet Candidates catalogues. The analysis includes evaluation of statistical probabilities, as well as checks on stellar binarity indicators such as the Renormalized Unit Weight Error (RUWE) and the IPD_frac_multi_peak values from Gaia Data Release 3. Our analysis validated 56 candidates as planets, identified 29 false positives, and classified 35 as likely planets. These results are intended to support the Ariel mission by providing a probabilistic priority ranking to guide follow-up observations and optimise targets for atmospheric characterisation studies.

Magellan PFS Discovery of a Cold Gas Giant Exoplanet Likely Formed by a Gas Disk Gravitational Instability

Alan P. Boss, R. Paul Butler, Johanna K. Teske, Guang-Yao Xiao et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究通过麦哲伦PFS光谱仪的16年观测数据,发现了环绕K7V型矮星HIP88962的冷气态巨行星,其轨道周期约19.3年,真实质量约4.83木星质量,半长轴约6.15天文单位,离心率0.718。结合Hipparcos和Gaia天体测量数据确定该行星轨道几乎是边缘倾视的(倾角91°±22°),其质量和轨道参数与核心吸积模型不符,但与气体盘引力不稳定性形成机制相一致。该发现为气体盘引力不稳定性作为质量较大气态巨行星的可行形成机制提供了观测证据。
英文原摘要
Abstract Using the Planet Finder Spectrograph (PFS) on the 6.5 m Magellan Clay Telescope at the Las Campanas Observatory, we have discovered a cold gas giant exoplanet orbiting the K7V dwarf star HIP88962. Sixteen years of PFS observations imply an exoplanet with an orbital period of ≈19.3 yr and a radial velocity semiamplitude (K) of 94.9 m s −1 . With a host star mass of 0.62 M ⊙ , this implies a planet mass M p sin( i ) ∼ 5 M Jup . While stable M dwarfs have a PFS rms of ≈3 m s −1 , the single Keplerian fit for HIP88962 has an rms of ≈6 m s −1 , hinting at a second exoplanet. The best double planet Keplerian fit, with a 4.113 day period, still results in an rms of ≈5 m s −1 . HIP88962 has Ca II H lines with twice the emission of four stable stars, implying that stellar activity may contribute to the rms jitter error for the single Keplerian fit. When combined with Hipparcos and Gaia astrometry, HIP88962 b has a nearly edge-on orbit with inclination 91° ± 22°, true planet mass 4.83 M Jup , semimajor axis 6.15 ±0.5 au, and eccentricity 0.718 ±0.02. Including a PFS jitter of ≈7 m s –1 yields χ 2 = 0.995 for the combined solution. A massive gas giant orbiting at ≈6 au around a 0.62 M ⊙ star is incompatible with published core accretion population synthesis models but consistent with formation by gas disk gravitational instability.
radial velocity · astrometry

Observational diagnostics of external photoevaporation at millimetre wavelengths

Luke Keyte, Thomas J. Haworth, Tyger Peake, Alvaro Ribas · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过大规模热化学模型网格预测了ALMA可观测的48条分子谱线在不同恒星和盘性质及外部FUV辐射强度(100-100,000 G0)下的流量,系统评估了这些谱线及其比值作为外部光蒸发诊断工具的有效性。研究发现单条谱线流量存在简并性,但精心选择的谱线比值(如同种分子的不同激发态比值或来自不同空间区域的物种比值)能有效消除这些简并,其中135个比值被认为具有诊断潜力。这项研究为通过毫米波观测识别受外部光蒸发影响的原行星盘及约束其外部FUV辐射强度提供了可操作的框架。
英文原摘要
External photoevaporation is increasingly recognised as an important influence on protoplanetary disk evolution. However, there are no established molecular line diagnostics that can reliably distinguish external irradiation from variations in intrinsic stellar and disk properties. We use a large grid of thermochemical models to predict the fluxes of 48 transitions from eight species observable with ALMA, spanning a wide range of system properties and external FUV fields of 100 to 100,000 G0. At fixed stellar and disk properties, all transitions generally brighten with increasing FUV field strength. HCO+ and CO fluxes increase monotonically in virtually all disks, whereas lower-excitation lines, particularly those of C2H and N2H+, respond weakly and non-monotonically. However, variations in stellar mass and disk size introduce substantial degeneracies, limiting the usefulness of individual line fluxes as tracers of external irradiation. We therefore assess all pairwise line flux ratios, which can significantly reduce these degeneracies, evaluating their correlation with the external field, the magnitude of their change across the FUV range, and predicted ALMA detectability. We identify 135 promising ratios, including several combining strong FUV sensitivity and high detectability with efficient single-tuning ALMA observations. The strongest diagnostics either contrast high- and low-excitation lines of the same or co-spatial species (e.g. CO J=8-7/J=1-0), probing different regions of the photoevaporative wind, or pair an FUV-sensitive wind tracer with comparatively insensitive emission arising closer to the disk (e.g. HCO+ J=3-2/C2H N=3-2). Our results provide a framework for identifying externally photoevaporating systems from spatially unresolved emission and constraining their incident FUV field strengths.
protoplanetary disk · disk evolution

A novel method for dust evolution in protoplanetary disks and a new criterion for gap opening

SM Gopika, Paul C. Duffell · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究提出了粒子驻留时间方法(GRTM),这一计算高效的粒子追踪方法通过重构粒子驻留时间来获得原行星盘中的尘埃径向分布,无需直接演化流体形式的尘埃。通过与FARGO3D标准方法的对比验证,我们建立了一个关于尘埃间隙开启的经验判据,揭示了小颗粒(St≲10⁻²)和亚木星质量行星条件下间隙形成的新机制,这对理解ALMA观测到的间隙具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
We present the Grain Residence-Time Method (GRTM), a computationally efficient particle-tracking approach for studying dust evolution and trapping in protoplanetary disks. By reconstructing steady-state dust distributions from particle residence times, this method provides a way to obtain radial dust surface density profiles without directly evolving the dust as a fluid. We compare the GRTM with dust profiles computed via standard methods using the $\texttt{FARGO3D}$ code, which models the dust as a pressureless fluid, and find very good agreement over a wide range of parameter space. Finally, we investigate how dust gap formation depends on the planet-to-star mass ratio ($q$) and Stokes number ($\mathrm{St}$), through a parameter study, establishing an empirical criterion for dust gap opening. In doing so, we have discovered a new regime of gap opening for small grains with $St \lesssim 10^{-2}$ and for planet mass well below Jupiter's, which is likely very relevant for gaps seen in ALMA images.
protoplanetary disk

Planet Formation in a Warped Disc: Gravitational Instability and Misaligned Infall

Sahl Rowther, Josh Calcino, Richard Alexander · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过三维SPH模拟研究了错位吸积对引力不稳定原行星盘的影响，发现错位吸积导致盘产生翘曲结构，外盘受到的加热和扭矩作用强于内盘。吸积气体一方面通过辐射加热增强盘的引力稳定性，另一方面持续增加盘质量并加强引力不稳定性，两种效应的竞争最终导致盘在翘曲结构中的旋臂碎裂形成巨行星。该研究表明吸积特性对引力不稳定性的影响取决于多个因素的平衡，并揭示了错位吸积在散射光、连续谱和分子线发射中引入的新的非对称特征。
英文原摘要
We perform three-dimensional smoothed particle hydrodynamics simulations to investigate the impact of misaligned infall on a gravitationally unstable protoplanetary disc. The interaction between the disc and the infalling gas does not occur uniformly across all radii. As infall impacts the outer disc first, the outer disc experiences stronger torques than the inner disc, resulting in a warped disc where the angular momentum vector of the disc varies with radius. The surrounding infalling gas reprocesses stellar irradiation and re-emits it back towards the disc, resulting in a warmer disc. The higher temperatures cause the disc to become more gravitationally stable resulting in weaker spiral arms. However, over time, infall also continuously increases the disc mass, gradually altering the local conditions and strengthening gravitational instabilities despite the azimuthally averaged Toomre $Q$ indicating that the disc is stable against gravitational instability. Eventually, one of the spiral arms fragments, forming a giant planet by gravitational instability in a disc warped by misaligned infall. Hence, the impact of infall on gravitational instability in general will vary depending on the infall and disc properties due to the balance between the stabilising and destabilising effects. We also find that infall alters the expected signatures of gravitationally unstable discs by introducing asymmetries in scattered-light, continuum, and molecular line emission.
planet formation · protoplanetary disc

3D and 2D Radiation Non-ideal MHD modeling of the decay of FUor outbursts

Yisheng Tu, Lee Hartmann, Zhaohuan Zhu, Conel M. O'D. Alexander et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过三维辐射非理想磁流体动力学模拟研究了磁旋转不稳定性驱动的FUor爆发衰减过程,发现衰减期间内盘形成的环隙结构中,磁通量优先集中在间隙内,而活跃的磁旋转不稳定环充当"磁坝"作用,通过非理想磁流体效应逐步释放磁通量并可能引发局部再激活事件。对比二维和三维模拟表明三维中磁流体加热更高效,导致爆发持续时间更长,且衰减时间主要取决于盘表面密度和中心星距离而对磁场强度依赖较弱,建立了爆发持续时间的经验关系。
英文原摘要
FU Orionis (FUor) outbursts are high-accretion events in protoplanetary disks thought to be driven by the magnetorotational instability (MRI). Although these outbursts span a wide range of observed durations and can thermally process and potentially transport disk material, the physical processes governing their decay remain poorly understood. We present a three-dimensional radiative non-ideal magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) simulation of the decay of an MRI-driven FUor outburst, complemented by a suite of two-dimensional models that explore its dependence on disk properties. During the decay, rings and gaps form naturally within the inner disk. Magnetic flux preferentially concentrates in the gaps, while the rings remain comparatively weakly magnetized. We show that MRI-active rings act as ``magnetic dams,'' confining magnetic flux within adjacent gaps. As the rings cool below the MRI activation temperature, non-ideal MHD effects decouple the gas from the magnetic field, allowing the trapped flux to diffuse outward. This outward diffusion can sometimes transiently reactivate MRI in neighboring rings, producing localized reheating events that we term ``Reactivation.'' By comparing otherwise identical two- and three-dimensional simulations, we find that MRI heating is systematically less efficient in two dimensions, leading to outburst durations shorter by a factor of $\sim3$--4. Our model suite further shows that the decay timescale depends primarily on the disk surface density and distance from the central star, with only a weak dependence on the magnetic field strength. We further derive an empirical relation linking the outburst duration to these quantities.
protoplanetary disk

Rapid Protoplanetary Disk Evolution and Debris Disk Formation in Taurus-Auriga

Joshua B. Lovell, Sean M. Andrews, Grant M. Kennedy, David J. Wilner et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过SMA 1.3mm连续波观测调查了金牛座-御夫座地区149个II类和82个III类年轻恒星的原行星盘演化，发现从II类到III类阶段的演化过程中超过99.5%的尘埃质量被耗散或转化，表明内外尘埃储层在可比时间尺度上快速演化。建立的毫米光度和尘埃盘质量分布模型证明，这些演化特征与从II类盘高效形成类木行星小体（形成效率几分之几至几十分之几）并最终演化为碎片盘的过程相符。
英文原摘要
We present a Submillimeter Array (SMA) 1.3mm (225GHz) continuum emission survey of Young Stellar Objects (YSOs) in the Taurus-Auriga star-forming region, comprising 69 class III and 5 class I/II YSOs with spectral types B9-M3, as part of a total sample of 149 class II and 82 class III YSOs. This represents a complete sample (100%) of class II and III YSOs with spectral types earlier than K3, and a near-complete sample (85%) for spectral types earlier than M2. The survey has a median $3σ$ sensitivity of 1.4mJy. We did not find any emission from circumstellar dust around class III YSOs, but present three detections that can be attributed to variable stellar activity, and one from an unresolved companion. We compile and model spectral energy distributions (SEDs) for the sample, and report 27 class III YSOs with excess thermal emission. We demonstrate that the class III YSOs in Taurus are typically older than their class II counterparts. We produce millimeter luminosity and dust disk mass distributions, and present evidence for a bifurcation between the class II and III populations, implying that ${\gtrsim}99.5\%$ of the dust mass is dispersed or transformed as YSOs transition from the class II to III stage. The combined infrared and millimeter properties demonstrate that warm (inner) and cold (outer) dust reservoirs evolve on comparable timescales: there is a rapid evolution across the radial extent of disks. We demonstrate that the survey properties are broadly consistent with a belt population that formed planetesimals from class II disks with an efficiency of a few tenths to a few tens of percent.

Connecting Calcium Depletion and Dust Evolution in the Rapid Accretor T28

Eylul Galimidi, Nuria Calvet, Marbely Micolta, Antonio Frasca et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过多波段观测和光谱建模分析了高吸积星T28的高度钙耗尽原行星盘的尘埃演化特征，发现该盘具有前过渡盘结构，由间隙分隔的两个光学厚盘组成。研究表明T28的钙耗尽程度与质量吸积率呈正相关，这一趋势可由大质量盘中优先进行的巨行星形成所解释，巨行星将难熔物质截留在盘内，导致到达恒星的物质中钙含量降低。
英文原摘要
We present a multi-wavelength case study of the star T28 in the Chamaeleon I star-forming region and investigate the dust evolution of its highly Ca-depleted protoplanetary disk. We combine new observations from the MIKE spectrograph on the Magellan Clay Telescope with archival IRS spectrum and photometry. We constrain the optical extinction using the hydrogen column density of the region, and apply ROTFIT to obtain stellar parameters. We estimate the mass accretion rate by fitting the excess flux above the photosphere with an accretion shock model. We model the line profiles of Ca II K and H$α$ with magnetospheric accretion models to infer Ca/H abundance, while the infrared excess is modeled with irradiated accretion disk models to get disk properties. We find that T28 is a high accretor with significant Ca depletion in its accretion flows. We find that the disk is best fit with a pre-transitional model with a disk mass of 0.12 $M_{\odot}$, where two optically thick disks are separated by a gap filled with optically thin dust extending to 16 au. This gap would not be resolved in the existing observations. T28 follows the trend of increasing depletion with increasing mass accretion rate. This trend can potentially be explained by preferential and early giant planet formation in massive disks, as suggested by recent planet formation simulations, leading to enhanced capture of refractory material in the massive disks of high accretors and thus depletion in the material reaching the star.

MINDS: The mid-infrared spectrum of the disk, jets, and winds of DG Tau

Manuel Güdel, Péter Ábrahám, Till Kaeufer, Ágnes Kóspál et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究利用詹姆斯·韦伯空间望远镜的中红外积分视场分光仪观测了原行星盘系统DG Tau，通过光谱分析和物理模型研究了原行星盘的尘埃矿物成分、气体温度结构以及双向喷流的电离和动力学性质。研究发现该系统的内盘存在显著的尘埃快速增长（≥2 μm）和极端的水/CO温度（600-2000 K），反映了强吸积驱动的恒星紫外辐照，而喷流中的金属离子电离则来自于非平衡电离激波冷却过程而非恒星高能辐射。这些观测结果揭示了原行星盘早期演化阶段的快速行星形成过程及喷流加热的物理机制。
英文原摘要
DG Tau is one of the best-studied young stellar objects in transition from a Class I protostar to a T Tauri star. It is no longer embedded in a dense molecular cloud, but still reveals features that are commonly seen in protostars at earlier evolutionary stages. Apart from a protoplanetary disk, DG Tau hosts two energetic jets that can be studied down to the immediate environment of the star and disk at optical, infrared, and X-ray wavelengths. We use the Medium Resolution Spectrometer (MRS), an integral field unit of MIRI on the Space Telescope (JWST), to study the gas and dust composition of the protoplanetary disk and investigate the dynamics and thermal evolution of the jet system. James Webb We studied the mineralogical composition of the disk with models including the hot inner rim of the disk, the disk midplane, and the dusty disk atmosphere. The gas disk composition is retrieved after spectral continuum subtraction, using slab models in local thermodynamic equilibrium. To study the ionization, thermal properties, and dynamics of the jets, we used spectral imaging across the entire MRS field of view and employed 1D shock models to study the emission and line ratios for ionized neon. i , H_2, and lines from ions such as Ne + $, Ne^2+, Ar^+, Fe^+, and Ni^+; no lines from any other molecules are detected. Our dust analysis reveals substantial grain growth to at least 2 $μm. Lines of H_2O and CO are formed at extremely high temperatures, in the range of 600-2000 K. The ratio of warm to hot water is the lowest among previous observations of disks with JWST. The pair of jets reveals a rich spectrum of emission lines from ions, in particular producing forbidden lines of ii , iii , ii , ii , and ii . The forward jet shows a collimated structure as found previously, while the hitherto poorly described, receding jet is more widely dispersed on scales of several arcseconds, showing a layered structure with the fastest components being the most collimated. Substantial grown dust in the inner disk of DG Tau at an early evolutionary stage points at rapid grain growth. Extreme water and CO temperatures in the inner disk and a very strong continuum point toward the relevance of the strong accretion of DG Tau and consequently strong stellar UV irradiation. In contrast, the ionization of the jet gas cannot be reconciled with high-energy radiation from the star itself. Instead, we propose a unified nonequilibrium ionization shock model in which the previously studied X-ray jet plasma continuously cools, recombining ions until, at temperatures around 10^4 K and below, metals at low ionization stages emit the observed ion lines.
protoplanetary disk

Beyond pebble isolation. Diverse pathways to giant planet formation across stellar and orbital scales

Lorenzo Peerani, Sho Shibata, Ravit Helled · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过数值模拟研究了不同恒星质量（0.1-1.5太阳质量）和轨道位置下的行星微行星吸积过程，探讨巨行星在盘寿命内到达临界质量的多样化途径。研究发现冷木星和温木星能够形成，而原地形成在近日轨道处失败，热木星的形成需要迁移解释；在远日轨道处，包层收缩效率高可在微行星隔离质量前达到临界质量。结果表明微行星吸积支持多条巨行星形成路径，包括极小核心的巨行星，不同形成条件可解释系外行星成分和内部结构的多样性。
英文原摘要
Giant planet formation requires reaching crossover mass, i.e. the point when the mass of the gaseous envelope becomes equal to the mass of the core, within the disc's lifetime. The formation process depends critically on the orbital distance and stellar mass. We aim to simulate planet formation via pebble accretion up to crossover mass around stellar hosts with masses of 0.1--1.5,M_⊙ considering a range of formation locations, with and without Type I migration. We used a modified version of that includes pebble accretion, gas accretion, and disc evolution. MESA We find that cold and warm Jupiters form, whereas in situ formation fails at short orbital separations; viscous heating raises the isolation mass enough to assemble adequate cores, but the accompanying high disc temperature prevents cooling and suppresses gas-giant formation. This supports migration-based explanations for the origin of hot Jupiters. At large orbital distances, crossover can be reached before pebble isolation mass. This is possible due to efficient envelope contraction in the cold, low-opacity outer disc. Inferred core masses at crossover range between about 1 and 20,M_⊕. Pebble accretion accommodates multiple formation pathways. Giant planets can also have very small cores. Overall, different formation conditions significantly influence planetary growth and can explain the diversity in compositions and internal structures observed in the exoplanet population.
